### [1] [Attention as Frustrated Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18694)
*Joshua Nunley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出受挫同步网络（FSN），通过将token状态表示为环面上的相位，并利用一个学习得到的复耦合核（包含谐波和一步延迟）来实现注意力机制。FSN将下一token预测建模为被数据“挫败”的同步过程，在百万参数级字符级文本和代码任务上，验证损失持续低于调优后的RoPE-SwiGLU Transformer，且优势随规模扩展至四百万参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全同步的振荡器网络无法进行进一步计算，因此基于同步的注意力架构必须在结构化的非一致状态中实现计算。

Method: FSN将token状态定义为环面上的相位（复相角），整个价值通路仅由一个学习得到的复耦合核（含谐波分量和一步延迟项）构成。谐波分量对应Kuramoto-Sakaguchi受挫角和Daido排斥分量，延迟项在代数上等价于以数据自身转移为受挫角的Kuramoto-Sakaguchi耦合，从而将下一token预测实现为被数据“挫败”的同步过程。

Result: 在百万参数级字符级文本和代码任务上，FSN的验证损失在每个测量epoch均低于调优后的RoPE-SwiGLU Transformer基线；FSN 30 epoch的enwik8种子结果均低于基线50 epoch收敛损失1.611，FSN 50 epoch收敛至1.5953±0.0014。用平均场耦合替代所有前馈层（无MLP）的变体仍可跟踪Transformer性能。在自然文本上，无受挫基座在所有复制深度上落后于收敛Transformer，而受挫核在4及以上深度逆转了该差距。优势在四百万参数规模下持续保持。

Conclusion: 受挫同步网络通过结构化的非一致同步状态（受挫）来实现注意力计算，在百万参数级任务上优于Transformer基线，证明了将同步理论中的受挫概念引入注意力架构的有效性。

Abstract: A network of oscillators that synchronizes perfectly computes nothing further, so an attention architecture built from synchronization must locate its computation in structured departures from agreement. We introduce the Frustrated Synchronization Network (FSN), whose token states are phases on a torus and whose entire value pathway is one learned complex coupling kernel over harmonics and a one-step delay. Each component of the kernel is a frustration in the sense of the synchronization literature. The complex phases are static Kuramoto-Sakaguchi frustration angles, the signed harmonics are repulsive Daido components, and the delay term, which couples each token to the successors of the tokens it attends to, is algebraically identical to Kuramoto-Sakaguchi coupling whose frustration angle is the data's own transition, so next-token prediction is implemented as synchronization frustrated by the data. At matched one-million-parameter and training budgets on character-level text and code, the FSN's validation loss is below a tuned RoPE-SwiGLU transformer's at every epoch measured, and the comparison survives training the baseline to convergence: every thirty-epoch enwik8 seed finishes below the transformer's converged fifty-epoch loss of 1.611, and the FSN's completed fifty-epoch runs converge to 1.5953 +/- 0.0014. A variant with every feed-forward block replaced by mean-field coupling to learned collective modes, leaving no multilayer perceptron in the stack, tracks the transformer. On natural text the unfrustrated base layer falls behind the converged transformer at every copy depth, worst on long-range copy events; the kernel reverses the deficit at every depth of four and beyond. Headline comparisons are at the one-million-parameter scale; a scale ladder is complete through four million parameters with the advantage persisting, and remaining arms are marked as in progress.

</details>


### [2] [FoMoE: Breaking the Full-Replica Barrier with a Federation of MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19025)
*Lorenzo Sani,Zeyu Cao,Meghdad Kurmanji,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanovic,Yan Gao,Wanru Zhao,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoMoE通过将专家层分区到不同工作节点上，打破了分布式训练中全模型复制的范式，在跨数据中心弱连接场景下显著降低通信开销并提升训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨数据中心分布式训练方法（如DiLoCo、Photon）要求每个站点维护完整的模型副本，导致巨大的内存约束和通信开销，无法高效扩展至大规模MoE模型。

Method: 提出FoMoE系统，核心思想是将MoE中的专家层（expert layers）分区部署到不同工作节点上，而非复制完整模型；同时引入skip-token机制进一步提升吞吐量。

Result: 相较于高效基线降低通信成本1.42倍，相较于DDP降低45.44倍；通过skip-token机制实现最高1.4倍的吞吐量加速；在100B参数规模配置下通过系统建模验证了通信和内存优势。

Conclusion: FoMoE验证了在跨数据中心弱连接环境下，通过专家层分区可以打破全模型复制范式，实现更高效的分布式MoE训练，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Pre-training Large Language Models (LLMs) typically demands large-scale infrastructure with tightly coupled hardware accelerators. While increasing model and dataset scale remains the dominant driver of performance, Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) architectures have recently achieved state-of-the-art results by decoupling parameter count from computational cost. This efficiency enables training massive models on constrained compute budgets, yet it typically requires the high-speed interconnects of a single datacenter. To overcome these physical limits, recent approaches such as DiLoCo and Photon use low-communication data-parallel methods to enable scaling across geographically distributed, weakly connected data centers. However, these methods suffer from a fundamental inefficiency: they require full model replicas at every site, which imposes prohibitive memory constraints and communication overheads. In this work, we introduce FoMoE, a system that breaks the full-replica paradigm by partitioning expert layers across workers. We demonstrate that FoMoE: (I) reduces communication costs by up to 1.42x over efficient baselines and 45.44x over DDP via partial expert replication in the studied regimes; (II) achieves empirical throughput speedups of up to 1.4x through a novel skip-token mechanism; and (III) shows stable routing in the trained proxy regimes and projects the communication/memory benefits to 100B-scale configurations through system modelling.

</details>


### [3] [Gaussian Mixture Attention: Linear-Time Sequence Mixing via Probabilistic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18283)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯混合注意力（GMA），一种概率式注意力机制，通过K个高斯混合组件将查询-键路由到共享隐空间，避免显式O(N²)点积交互，实现固定K下O(NK)的线性复杂度，在长上下文中与基线方法表现竞争力，但尚未超越优化的softmax注意力或状态空间模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力的密集token-to-token交互模式是Transformer扩展到长上下文的核心瓶颈，需要设计一种避免显式N×N亲和矩阵计算的线性复杂度注意力替代方案。

Method: GMA将查询和键映射到K个高斯混合组件上的后验责任向量，通过责任向量间的重叠定义隐式亲和度；值通过K槽潜存进行读写。利用矩阵乘法结合律，仅存储两个O(NK)的责任矩阵而非O(N²)的亲和矩阵。设计了双向和因果变体，并提供了端到端可微的参数化方案。

Result: GMA实现固定K线性内存缩放，在长上下文分类任务上与注意力基线相当；因果GMA在WikiText-103上优于测试的线性/随机特征注意力变体，但落后于优化的因果SDPA和Mamba。责任分析显示组件被广泛使用且与表层词类别有一定对齐。

Conclusion: GMA是一种概率性、可解释、固定K线性时间的注意力替代方案，但并非优化softmax注意力或状态空间模型的通用替代品。

Abstract: The dense token-to-token interaction pattern of standard dot-product attention remains a central bottleneck in scaling Transformer architectures to long contexts. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Mixture Attention (GMA)}, a probabilistic attention-style sequence mixer that replaces explicit pairwise query--key comparison with routing through $K$ learned Gaussian mixture components. Queries and keys are mapped to posterior \textit{responsibility} vectors over a shared latent routing space; their overlap defines an implicit responsibility-space affinity, while values are written into and read from a $K$-slot latent memory. By exploiting the associativity of matrix multiplication, GMA avoids materializing the induced $N\times N$ affinity matrix and instead uses two responsibility matrices whose dominant activation storage scales as $\mathcal{O}(NK)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ for fixed $K$. We formulate bidirectional and causal variants of GMA, provide an end-to-end differentiable parameterization of the Gaussian mixture components, and analyze its responsibility-modulated gradient structure, constrained non-negative low-rank affinity interpretation, and local routing stability. Empirically, GMA exhibits the intended fixed-$K$ linear memory scaling and is competitive with attention-style baselines on long-context classification, while causal GMA improves over tested linear/random-feature attention variants on WikiText-103 but remains behind optimized causal SDPA and Mamba in the current implementation. Analysis of learned responsibilities further shows broad component usage and moderate alignment with surface-form token categories, supporting GMA as a probabilistic, interpretable, fixed-$K$ linear-time attention-style alternative rather than a universal replacement for optimized softmax attention or state-space models.

</details>


### [4] [OrthoReg: Orthogonal Regularization for Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19145)
*Till Richter,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合建模中神经网络可能重新学习机械部分导致冗余和不可解释的问题，提出OrthoReg（正交正则化）方法，通过直接惩罚符号组件与神经网络组件之间的重叠，实现互补分解，提升符号恢复和分布外性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合建模中神经网络组件可能重新学习已由符号（物理）组件捕捉的机械部分，导致模型冗余且不可解释，尤其在符号结构本身是从数据中发现时，基于标准L^2正则化的方法因投影论证失效而无法解决该问题。

Method: 提出OrthoReg（正交正则化），直接惩罚符号组件与神经网络组件之间的重叠，防止符号结构被神经残差吸收，实现符号部分捕捉库可表达内容、神经部分捕捉剩余内容的互补分解。

Result: 在存在部分库不匹配的基准动力系统上，OrthoReg改善了符号恢复和分布外行为。

Conclusion: OrthoReg通过正交正则化有效解决了混合建模中组件重叠问题，提升了模型的可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamical systems are fundamental to modeling the natural world, yet modeling them involves a persistent trade-off: manually prescribed mechanistic models are interpretable by design but often overly simplistic and misspecified; in contrast, flexible data-driven neural methods lack physical insight. Hybrid modeling aims for the best of both worlds by combining a prescribed or symbolic, physics-based component with a flexible neural network. A critical challenge, however, is that the neural component may relearn mechanistic parts, yielding redundant and uninterpretable models, especially when the symbolic structure itself is discovered from data. Existing methods based on standard $L^2$ regularization rely on a projection argument that breaks when the symbolic component is learned through sparse discovery, allowing the neural augmentation to overlap with symbolic structure. We introduce \textbf{OrthoReg} (Orthogonal Regularization), which directly penalizes overlap between the symbolic and neural components, preventing symbolic structure from being absorbed by the neural residual. This yields a complementary decomposition: the symbolic part captures what the library can express, and the neural part captures what remains. On benchmark dynamical systems with partial library mismatch, OrthoReg improves symbolic recovery and out-of-distribution behavior.

</details>


### [5] [Breaking the Solver Bottleneck: Training Task Generators at the Learnable Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18284)
*Lorenz Wolf,Connor Watts,Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew Daborn-Sargent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROPEL提出一种求解器摊销框架，通过轻量级激活探针预测生成任务的目标求解率，避免直接优化时需重复执行求解器的计算成本，从而高效训练任务生成器以产出处于可学习前沿的难度适中的任务。在数学、代码和软件工程任务中，该方法显著提升了处于目标求解率水平的任务生成比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中智能体训练的资源瓶颈日益转向前沿任务供给——即有效、可解且难度刚好适配当前模型的任务。固定任务分布会随模型提升而饱和，而朴素合成生成的任务要么过于简单、要么不可能解决、要么不适定。直接使用RL优化任务生成器需要为每个候选任务重复执行求解器，对于软件工程任务，单次求解器执行需数十分钟，求解器在环的生成器训练不可行。

Method: PROPEL框架：首先构建一次性标注语料库，包含生成的任务及其求解器结果；然后在该语料上训练一个轻量级激活探针（activation probe），该探针从冻结的生成器参考模型中预测目标求解器的通过率；在生成器优化阶段，用探针作为求解率的代理，将生成器评估简化为单次前向传播，从而避免昂贵的求解器在环训练。

Result: 在编程任务中，可学习前沿的任务生成比例从10.1%提升至20.0%（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct求解器），从5.3%提升至12.6%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct求解器）。在软件工程任务中，目标求解率水平的生成比例从9.8%提升至19.6%（Qwen3.5-27B，探针和生成器训练时未见过的仓库）。

Conclusion: PROPEL通过求解器摊销策略有效解决了任务生成器训练中求解器在环的计算瓶颈，在多种模型规模和任务领域（数学、代码、软件工程）均能显著将生成任务向目标求解率偏移，证明了该框架的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: The limiting resource for training agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly frontier task supply: valid, solvable tasks just difficult enough to train the current model. As reasoning and agentic models improve, fixed task distributions saturate, while naive synthetic generation yields tasks that are trivial, impossible, or ill-posed. Training a task generator with RL to optimize validity and learnability can address this bottleneck, but direct optimization requires repeated solver rollouts per candidate. For software-engineering (SWE) tasks, a single rollout can take tens of minutes; solver-in-the-loop generator training is intractable. We introduce PROPEL, a solver-amortized framework for training task generators at the targeted solve rate. PROPEL trains a lightweight activation probe on a one-time labeled corpus of generated tasks and solver outcomes. The probe predicts target-solver pass rate from a frozen generator reference model and serves as a proxy for solve rate during generator optimization, reducing generator evaluation to a single forward pass. Across math, code, and software-engineering at multiple model scales, PROPEL shifts generation toward the targeted solve rate: for coding, tasks generated at the learnable frontier increase from $10.1\% \rightarrow 20.0\%$ for a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct solver and from $5.3\% \rightarrow 12.6\%$ for a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct solver. For SWE, PROPEL increases the share of generations at the targeted solve rate from $9.8\% \rightarrow 19.6\%$ for Qwen3.5-27B on repositories not seen during training of probe and generator.

</details>


### [6] [CODEBLOCK: Learning to Supervise Code at the Right Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18286)
*Zhijie Deng,Ling Li,Jinlong Pang,Kaiqin Hu,Qi Xuan,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeBlock提出了一种结构感知的稀疏监督框架，通过选择结构完整的代码块而非孤立token进行监督，在仅使用1.9%响应token的情况下，在六个代码生成基准上取得了优于全token SFT的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码LLM的监督微调对所有响应token使用统一的交叉熵损失，但代码中的token对学习的贡献不同；而直接将NLP领域的token级掩码方法迁移到代码领域会破坏代码的语法和语义完整性。

Method: 首先筛选高质量指令-响应对，然后将代码响应划分为语法连贯的编码项，通过聚合核心逻辑token的广义交叉熵估计其效用，再使用数据流可达性和桥接信号进行重排序以优先选择传播重要程序依赖的块。训练时完整响应作为上下文，但仅对选中的代码项和信息性NL token计算损失。

Result: 在六个代码生成基准上，CodeBlock在pass@1指标上优于全token SFT和竞争性基线方法，同时仅使用了1.9%的监督响应token。

Conclusion: 结构感知的稀疏监督能够有效提升代码LLM微调效率，证明了保留代码结构完整性的重要性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of code LLMs typically applies uniform cross-entropy loss to all response tokens, implicitly assuming that every token provides equally useful learning signal. Recent token-level selection methods challenge this assumption in natural-language SFT by supervising only high-value tokens. However, directly transferring token-level masking to code can break syntactically and semantically coherent program units, because code depends on structural completeness and definition-use relations. We therefore propose CodeBlock, a structure-aware sparse supervision framework that selects structure-complete code evidence rather than isolated tokens. CodeBlock first selects high-quality instruction-response pairs, then partitions code responses into syntactically coherent coding items, estimates their utility by aggregating generalized cross-entropy over core logic tokens, and reranks them with data-flow reach and bridge signals to prioritize blocks that propagate or connect important program dependencies. During training, the full response remains available as context, while loss is applied only to selected code items and informative natural-language tokens. Experiments on six code-generation benchmarks show that CodeBlock achieves stronger average pass@1 than full-token SFT and competitive selection baselines, while using only 1.9% of supervised response tokens.

</details>


### [7] [Artemis: Anatomy-Resolved inTervention for Eliminating Multimodal NeuroImage confounderS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18287)
*Siyuan Dai,Yang Du,Kun Zhao,Zhusuyi Chen,Heng Huang,Paul Thompson,Chao Shi,Haoteng Tang,Liang Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Artemis框架，通过逐脑区因果干预学习区域特异性混杂表示，解决多模态脑网络中人口统计学混杂因素导致的GNN虚假关联问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果GNN方法在处理临床神经影像数据时，未能考虑年龄、性别等真实世界混杂因素对脑连接组与临床结局关系的系统干扰，且缺乏针对不同脑区混杂敏感性的区域级调整。

Method: 提出Artemis区域级因果框架，对每个脑区独立进行因果干预，学习轻量化的区域特异性混杂表示，利用多模态功能与结构特征进行图推理，作为兼容任意GNN骨干的即插即用模块。

Result: 在ADNI（疾病诊断）、OASIS（痴呆分期）、HCP（性别分类）三个基准数据集上持续优于代表性GNN基线，多项支持实验验证了统计显著性和神经科学可解释性。

Conclusion: Artemis通过区域级因果干预有效缓解了人口统计学混杂因素对脑网络分析的干扰，同时保持了对不同GNN骨干的普适性和神经科学可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging, integrating functional connectivity from fMRI and structural connectivity from DTI, enables non-invasive analysis of brain networks using graph neural networks. However, demographic factors such as age and sex systematically confound the relationship between brain connectivity and clinical outcomes, causing GNNs to exploit spurious shortcuts rather than learning causally invariant representations. While recent causal GNN methods introduce causality at the graph-modeling level, their causal mechanisms remain domain-agnostic without accounting for the real-world confounders inherent in clinical neuroimaging data. Moreover, brain networks are constructed from atlas-based parcellations where each region exhibits distinct sensitivity to demographic factors, necessitating region-aware adjustment. We propose Artemis, a region-level causal framework that bridges this gap with causal intervention at each brain region independently by learning region-specific confounder representations with lightweight parameters. Our adjustment comprehensively utilized the multimodal functional and structural features for graph reasoning as a plug-in module compatible with arbitrary GNN backbones. Experiments on three benchmarks, ADNI for disease diagnosis, OASIS for dementia staging, and HCP for sex classification, demonstrate consistent improvements over representative GNN-based baselines. Multiple supporting experiments further demonstrate statistical significance and neuroscientific interpretability.

</details>


### [8] [A Link between Shock-wave Theory and Symmetry-reduced Stochastic Gradient Descent for Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18303)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了冲击波理论与随机梯度下降对称商化学习动力学之间的数学联系，通过微分几何、李群理论和流体力学，推导出商流形上的粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程和Burgers型方程，并严格证明了冲击形成。该理论适用于MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络，并提出对称校正的商可观测量可作为深度学习实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立冲击波理论与SGD学习动力学之间严格的数学联系，揭示参数对称性对深度学习训练动力学的影响，并为训练相变提供可操作的诊断工具。

Method: 结合微分几何、李群理论和流体力学，对参数对称性进行商化处理，采用局部熵粗粒化方法，推导出商流形上的粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程和Burgers型方程，并严格证明冲击波形成条件。

Result: 证明了MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络均满足Hamilton-Jacobi或Burgers型方程；指出原始参数范数因对称性冗余而产生失真，而对称校正的商可观测量能更有效地监测、预测和控制训练相变。

Conclusion: 该框架为深度学习训练动力学提供了理论支撑，并有望成为实用的训练诊断工具，尤其适用于Transformer等现代架构。

Abstract: We develop a mathematically explicit link between shock-wave theory and the symmetry-quotiented learning dynamics of stochastic gradient descent, drawing on differential geometry, Lie group theory, and fluid mechanics. Specifically, after quotienting parameter symmetries and applying local-entropy coarse-graining, the effective dynamics satisfy a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation on the quotient manifold. Moreover, under the assumption that the raw parameter dynamics can be summarized by a gradient field on the quotiented space, the gradient of the coarse-grained loss function obeys a Burgers-type equation, and shock formation can be established rigorously. We apply our theory to multilayer perceptrons, convolutional neural networks, Transformers, and mean-field networks, and show that they obey the Hamilton--Jacobi or Burgers-type equations. We conjecture that this framework also yields practical diagnostics for deep learning. In architectures such as Transformers, raw parameter norms are often distorted by symmetry redundancy and may therefore be misleading, whereas symmetry-corrected quotient observables provide a principled basis for monitoring, forecasting, and controlling training-phase transitions.

</details>
