### [153] [Improving Human-Robot Teamwork in Urban Search and Rescue Through Episodic Memory of Prior Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18836)
*Taewoon Kim,Emma van Zoelen,Mark Neerincx*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何让机器人利用以往团队协作中发现的协作模式（CPs）作为知识图谱 episodic 记忆，通过图表示学习和节点分类选择最有效的记忆进行复用，从而在未来的新协作中初始化机器人。实验表明，该方法将搜索救援成功率从25.7%提升至41.3%，平均任务时间减少283秒，尤其在交互初期效果最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机团队协作中，机器人需要从交互一开始就适应合作伙伴、情境和任务动态。然而现有方法通常缺乏利用先前团队协作经验的能力，导致机器人在新交互起始阶段表现不佳。

Method: 在MATRX USAR环境中，通过聊天与反思界面让用户外化协作模式。将历史协作模式表示为知识图谱 episodic 记忆，采用图表示学习结合节点分类目标，识别出最具代表性和有效性的记忆以供复用。在新的协作回合开始前，用该记忆初始化机器人。

Result: 在20名参与者、160轮次观察中，使用自动选择的先前协作模式初始化机器人后，救援成功率从25.7%提升至41.3%，平均任务时间减少283秒。提升效果在交互初期最为显著。

Conclusion: 可复用的 episodic 记忆能够帮助机器人进入协作时携带更有效的任务知识，支持更顺畅的早期团队协作。

Abstract: Effective human-robot teamwork requires robots to adapt to partners, situations, and task dynamics from the start of an interaction. In the MATRX Urban Search and Rescue (USAR) environment, people can externalize collaboration patterns (CPs) they discover during teamwork through a chat and reflection interface. We study whether a robot can use such prior team experience to become a better teammate in future interactions. To this end, we represent historical CPs as knowledge-graph episodic memories and use graph representation learning with a node-classification objective to identify a representative and effective memory for reuse. We then initialize the robot with this memory before a new collaboration episode begins. Across 20 participants and 160 round-level observations, initializing the robot with a single automatically selected prior CP increases rescue success from 25.7% to 41.3% and reduces average task time by 283 seconds. The strongest gains appear at the beginning of interaction, suggesting that reusable episodic memory can help robots enter collaboration with more effective task knowledge and support smoother early teamwork.

</details>


### [154] [A Clinician-Centered Pipeline for Annotation and Evaluation in Ultrasound AI Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19174)
*Fangyijie Wang,Jianjun Yu,Wentao Shi,Haixia Huang,Ran Shi,Guénolé Silvestre,Kathleen M. Curran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向临床医生的远程标注与评估流程（SonoRate），支持超声AI研究中的盲法模型比较与可重复评估，并在胎儿超声分割研究中通过六名不同经验水平的评估者验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像平台主要聚焦于数据集标注，缺乏对盲法模型比较和可重复评估工作流的集成支持，而临床医生的评估对于验证医学AI系统的临床可用性至关重要，尤其在超声影像中定量指标往往无法完全反映临床实用性。

Method: 提出一个基于集中式服务器和轻量级浏览器界面的临床医生中心化流程，支持远程标注、盲法排名、多评估者参与、结果集中聚合和自动化统计分析，无需本地下载数据集。

Result: 在胎儿超声分割研究中，六名评估者（涵盖专家、普通医生和非专家）参与，系统自动生成Spearman相关、Kendall τ和top-1选择统计量，结果显示专家与其他组之间存在中等到强的一致性，盲法评估表明后期主动学习模型更受青睐。

Conclusion: 该流程能够支持超声影像中临床医生中心的标注和可重复的人-AI评估研究，代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Clinician-centered evaluation is critical for validating medical AI systems, especially in ultrasound imaging where quantitative metrics do not always capture clinical usability. Existing medical image platforms primarily focus on dataset labeling. They lack integrated support for blinded model comparison and reproducible evaluation workflows. We present a clinician-centered pipeline for remote annotation and evaluation in ultrasound AI studies. The proposed pipeline uses a centralized server and lightweight browser interfaces to enable clinicians to perform annotation, blinded ranking, and review without local dataset downloads. The pipeline also supports multi-rater participation, centralized result aggregation, and automated statistical analysis. We validate the pipeline in a fetal ultrasound segmentation study with six raters spanning expert, generalist, and non-expert experience levels. The system automatically generated Spearman correlation, Kendall's $τ$, and top-1 selection statistics. Results indicated moderate to strong agreement across experts and other groups. The blinded evaluation results showed a tendency for later active learning models to be preferred. These outcomes suggest that the pipeline can support clinician-centered annotation and reproducible human-\ac{AI} evaluation studies in ultrasound imaging. The proposed pipeline is available on \href{https://github.com/13204942/SonoRate}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [155] [A Taxonomy of Mental Health and Technology Needs for Alzheimer's and Dementia Caregivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19247)
*Keran Wang,Drishti Goel,Jiayue Melissa Shi,Violeta J. Rodriguez,Daniel S. Brown,Dong Whi Yoo,Ravi Karkar,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对AD/ADRD非正式照护者（"隐形第二患者"）的心理健康问题，提出了一种照护者心理健康与技术分类法，系统性地将照护者需求与各类技术干预相对应，识别照护优先级与现有技术支持之间的错配，并指出关系紧张和同情疲劳等未充分服务的领域，为临床、心理和技术领域提供了共享框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将照护者复杂的心理社会经验简化为单一的"照护者负担"概念，掩盖了哪些具体需求未得到满足或得到有效支持；同时数字和AI技术快速发展，但医学、心理学和技术研究之间缺乏共享框架，限制了累积性进展。

Method: 基于跨学科文献综述和两项针对照护者的定性研究，构建照护者心理健康与技术分类法。

Result: 识别出照护者优先级与现有技术支持之间的错配；发现关系紧张和同情疲劳等未得到充分服务的领域；提出了适应性、响应式系统的设计方向。

Conclusion: 该分类法提供了共享词汇，可指导临床医生、研究人员和技术设计师开发更加以人为本、临床基础扎实的痴呆症照护创新。

Abstract: Family members caring for individuals with Alzheimer's disease and related dementias (AD/ADRD) provide the foundation of long-term care worldwide. In 2023, more than 11 million U.S. family and friends contributed 18 billion hours of unpaid care, often at the cost of their own physical and mental health. These informal caregivers -- also referred as the "invisible second patients" -- experience elevated rates of mental health problems. Yet research commonly reduces their complex psychosocial experiences to a single construct of caregiver burden, obscuring which specific needs are unmet or effectively supported. At the same time, digital and AI-enabled technologies are rapidly expanding, from smartphone apps and videoconferencing to sensor platforms and AI chatbots. However, the absence of shared frameworks across medicine, psychology, and technology research limits cumulative progress. This study introduces a Caregiver Mental Health and Technology Taxonomy that systematically links AD/ADRD caregiver needs with corresponding classes of technology-based interventions. Drawing from an interdisciplinary literature review and two qualitative studies with caregivers, the taxonomy identifies mismatches between caregiver priorities and existing technological support, highlights under-served domains such as relational strain and compassion fatigue, and proposes design directions for adaptive, responsive systems. The framework offers a shared vocabulary to guide clinicians, researchers, and technology designers in developing more person-centered and clinically grounded innovation in dementia care.

</details>


### [156] [Correct Yourself, Keep My Trust: How Self-Correction and Social Connection Shape Credibility in Social Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19286)
*Biswadeep Sen,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 社交聊天机器人犯错后，自我纠错能在不损害可信度的前提下有效纠正错误，而用户与机器人的社会连接强度仅在自我纠错时能显著预测信念改变幅度；外包纠错会切断这一联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交聊天机器人日益普及但易产生错误信息，错误恢复策略对用户信任至关重要，然而不同纠错策略对聊天机器人可信度及社会连接作用机制的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用120名被试的组间实验，比较三种纠错策略：网页撤回、同一社交聊天机器人自我纠错、专家聊天机器人纠错。

Result: 三种策略均能有效纠正错误，但只有自我纠错不损害聊天机器人的可信度；社会连接强度仅在自我纠错时显著预测信念改变幅度，外包纠错则切断此关联。

Conclusion: 社交聊天机器人应自我纠错而非外包纠错，社会连接是增强纠错效果的功能性机制，而非单纯的设计特征。

Abstract: When social chatbots make mistakes, and they do, how they recover determines whether users trust them again. Social chatbots are increasingly integrated into everyday life, yet they remain prone to generating convincing but inaccurate information. The social connection they build with users makes such errors particularly consequential. We conducted a between-subjects experiment (N=120) comparing three error correction strategies: a webpage retraction, self-correction by the same social chatbot, and correction by an expert chatbot. Our results reveal two key findings. First, all three strategies corrected the error equally well, but only self-correction did so without damaging the chatbot's credibility: participants rated self-correcting chatbots significantly higher in both trustworthiness and perceived expertise than chatbots whose errors were corrected by external sources. Second, the strength of the user's social connection with the chatbot, measured through social attraction and self-disclosure, significantly predicted the magnitude of belief change, but only when the chatbot corrected itself. Outsourcing corrections to an external source severed this link entirely. These findings suggest that social chatbots should correct their own mistakes rather than outsource corrections, and that investing in social connection is a functional mechanism that amplifies correction effectiveness, not merely a design feature. We discuss implications for designing chatbots that maintain long-term credibility while effectively addressing their own errors.

</details>


### [157] [Integrating Multi-Label Classification and Generative AI for Scalable Analysis of User Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23018)
*Sandra Loop,Erik Bertram,Sebastian Juhl,Martin Schrepp*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在一家大型软件公司长期UX测量项目中开发的技术，包括使用监督机器学习对用户评论进行主题分类、利用生成式AI创建摘要，并发现情感分析不能可靠反映用户满意度，产品满意度需要显式测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞争激烈的软件市场中，用户体验评估对确保软件质量和长期产品成功至关重要。虽然开放式反馈提供了有价值的见解，但分析大量用户评论具有挑战性且耗时。

Method: 采用监督机器学习为评论分配预定义主题标签；利用生成式AI创建用户反馈的简洁摘要；同时研究了评论情感与整体产品满意度之间的关系。

Result: 情感分析本身不能可靠反映用户满意度；产品满意度需要通过调查显式评估来测量用户对产品的感知。

Conclusion: 需要显式测量产品满意度而非依赖情感分析作为替代指标。

Abstract: In highly competitive software markets, user experience (UX) evaluation is crucial for ensuring software quality and fostering long-term product success. Such UX evaluations typically combine quantitative metrics from standardized questionnaires with qualitative feedback collected through open-ended questions. While open-ended feedback offers valuable insights for improvement and helps explain quantitative results, analyzing large volumes of user comments is challenging and time-consuming. In this paper, we present techniques developed during a long-term UX measurement project at a major software company to efficiently process and interpret extensive volumes of user comments. To provide a high-level overview of the collected comments, we employ a supervised machine learning approach that assigns meaningful, pre-defined topic labels to each comment. Additionally, we demonstrate how generative AI (GenAI) can be leveraged to create concise and informative summaries of user feedback, facilitating effective communication of findings to the organization and especially upper management. Finally, we investigate whether the sentiment expressed in user comments can serve as an indicator for overall product satisfaction. Our results show that sentiment analysis alone does not reliably reflect user satisfaction. Instead, product satisfaction needs to be assessed explicitly in surveys to measure the user's perception of the product.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Adaptive Speech-to-Spike Encoding for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19039)
*Taharim Rahman Anon,Jakaria Islam Emon*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可学习的残差语音到脉冲编码器，与R-LIF骨干网络端到端联合训练，在GSC-v2上达到94.97%的准确率，并通过分析表明编码器学习的是任务对齐的脉冲表示而非信号重建，同时对比了DFA与BPTT在神经形态语音处理中的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续声学信号与离散事件驱动处理之间的不匹配是神经形态语音处理的根本瓶颈。现有系统依赖固定的脉冲编码器，迫使下游SNN去补偿非自适应的输入表示，缺乏可学习的编码机制。

Method: 提出可学习的残差语音到脉冲编码器，与Recurrent Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (R-LIF)骨干网络进行端到端联合训练。采用线性探测和梯度残差分析等方法对编码器进行分析，并对比了Direct Feedback Alignment (DFA)与基于替代梯度的BPTT两种学习规则。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands v2上达到94.97%的准确率；35k参数的紧凑变体达到89.8%，以少一个数量级的参数匹配或超越先前基线；DFA达到91.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: 学习到的编码器不追求信号重建，而是学习任务对齐的脉冲表示以增强类别可分性；DFA作为一种生物启发的学习规则，在神经形态音频任务中能够达到接近BPTT的性能，量化了生物启发学习规则的性能权衡。

Abstract: The mismatch between continuous acoustic signals and discrete event-driven processing remains a fundamental bottleneck for neuromorphic speech processing. Current systems typically rely on fixed spike encoders, forcing downstream Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) to compensate for non-adaptive input representations. To address this, we present a learnable residual speech-to-spike encoder jointly trained end-to-end with a Recurrent Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (R-LIF) backbone. We validate this approach on the Google Speech Commands v2 (GSC-v2) benchmark, achieving up to 94.97% accuracy. Notably, the learned encoder remains highly parameter-efficient with a compact 35k-parameter variant that reaches 89.8%, matching or exceeding prior baselines that require an order of magnitude more parameters. Our encoder-focused analysis, including linear probing and gradient-residual inspection, indicates that the encoder does not target faithful signal reconstruction but instead learns task-aligned spike representations that enhance class separability. Finally, we benchmark bio-inspired, hardware-friendly credit assignment by comparing Direct Feedback Alignment (DFA) with surrogate-gradient BPTT under identical architectures and training conditions. We find that DFA reaches 91.5% accuracy, quantifying the performance trade-off of bio-inspired learning rules for modern neuromorphic audio.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [A physical adaptive material motor unit neural network: a hygromorph composite material machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18275)
*Charles de Kergariou,David Correa,Adam W. Perriman,Helmut Hauser,Fabrizio Scarpa*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于木材和碳黑复合材料的物理自适应材料马达单元神经网络，利用温湿度敏感致动器构建类似肌肉收缩触发机制的智能机器，实现动态遮阳控制。通过超过350个实验数据点训练神经网络，采用数据感知反向传播算法，使机器能够随数据库扩展逐步学习预测遮阳行为，并能在不同条件下优化配置以实现相似遮阳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用新型材料科学进展，将记忆和学习能力直接嵌入材料中，使结构能够作为智能机器运行，实现建筑动态遮阳控制等实际应用。

Method: 采用木材和碳黑基复合材料构建新型可控致动器，组装成类似肌肉收缩触发机制的马达单元结构；建立神经网络并采用数据感知反向传播训练算法，基于超过350个多样化环境条件下的实验数据点进行训练。

Result: 该机器能够预测遮阳响应，并随数据库扩展逐步学习预测适当行为，还能优化配置以在两种不同条件下实现相似的遮阳输出。

Conclusion: 通过将材料智能与神经网络相结合，实现了具有自主学习能力的物理智能机器，为建筑动态遮阳控制提供了新方案，展示了材料嵌入式智能的可行性。

Abstract: Advances in novel materials science enable structures to function as intelligent machines by embedding memory and learning capabilities directly into materials. Our work introduces a physical adaptive material motor unit neural network,leveraging a new generation of controllable actuators composed of wood- and carbon black-based composites, sensitive to temperature and relative humidity. These material actuators are assembled into a motor unit-like structure inspired by muscle contraction trigger, forming an intelligent machine capable of dynamic shading control that can be used, for example, in buildings. The machine is governed by a neural network trained on over 350 experimental data points collected under diverse environmental conditions. By establishing a new data-aware backpropagation training, we show that the machine predicts shading responses and learns to predict appropriate behaviour incrementally as the database expands. We also demonstrate the ability of the machine to optimise configurations to achieve similar shading outputs under two distinct conditions.

</details>


### [160] [Nanoscale memristive devices: Threats and solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18978)
*Amir M. Hajisadeghi,Javad Talafy,Hamid R. Zarandi*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本章聚焦于ReRAM和STT-RAM两种基于忆阻器的存储技术，介绍了它们在替代CMOS存储器方面的优势（非易失性、低泄漏、高密度），详细分析了其面临的可靠性威胁（读/写错误和软错误）及最新解决方案，并探讨了忆阻器在交叉阵列中作为计算单元实现逻辑与算术运算的新兴能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMOS存储器在深亚微米工艺下面临功能挑战，而忆阻器因其激励特性（非易失性、低泄漏、高密度）成为有前景的替代方案。其中ReRAM和STT-RAM在工艺成熟度和性能指标（能耗、延迟、面积）方面尤为突出，因此本文聚焦于这两种技术，旨在解决其可靠性问题并拓展其计算应用。

Method: 本文为综述性章节，首先将可靠性威胁分类为读/写错误和软错误两大类，然后全面描述这些威胁并提出当前最先进的解决方案，最后展示如何在交叉阵列中利用忆阻器执行逻辑与算术运算。

Result: 系统地总结了ReRAM和STT-RAM面临的主要可靠性威胁及其相互影响关系，归纳了当前最先进的解决方案，并展示了忆阻器在交叉阵列中实现计算功能（逻辑与算术运算）的可行性。

Conclusion: 忆阻器（特别是ReRAM和STT-RAM）有望替代CMOS存储器，但需解决其可靠性问题；同时忆阻器作为计算单元的潜力可最小化计算中的数据恢复开销，为未来存内计算架构提供新方向。

Abstract: Due to their incentivizing features, memristors are a promising candidate for replacing CMOS-based memories, which are faced with various functional challenges in deep submicron process technologies. Memristors are nonvolatile, have low leakage, and are dense in comparison to CMOS-based memories like SRAM. In this regard, resistive RAM (ReRAM) and spin-transfer-torque RAM (STT-RAM) memristors are distinguished among other memristor-based memory technologies, due to their superiority in process maturity and metrics such as memory operation energy, memory latency, and area. Hence, this chapter focuses on these two memristor-based memory technologies. Despite the good features of these types of memory, they suffer from some reliability threats. Reliability parameters affect each other, and examining their positive and negative effects has a significant impact on the effectiveness of the proposed solutions. In one view, the threats can be categorized into two classes: (1) read/write error and (2) soft error. In this chapter, we comprehensively describe these threats and present the state-of-the-art solutions that enable the widespread use of memristors, particularly ReRAM and STT-RAM, in different applications. Finally, we introduce the emerging ability of memristors as a computing unit aiming to minimize data restoration in computing, and we show how to perform logic and arithmetic computation in a crossbar array.

</details>


### [161] [Not Your Usual FFT: QFT$\rightarrow$FFT via Classical Quantum-Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18981)
*Stefano Markidis,Gilbert Netzer,Luca Pennati,Frej Larssen,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: QFT→FFT是一个通过量子傅里叶变换电路在经典量子计算机模拟器上计算离散傅里叶变换的HPC库族，在AMD EPYC Zen2上AVX性能与FFTW相当（64线程），在NVIDIA A100上CUDA后端比后者的性能提升4倍以上，并支持通过近似QFT截断小角度旋转来降低电路深度和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用量子傅里叶变换（QFT）的电路结构在经典高性能计算平台上实现离散傅里叶变换（DFT），以期获得计算性能上的优势或替代传统FFT库的新路径。

Method: 将输入数组直接映射到量子态振幅，设计后端无关的计划器构建融合门调度和内存布局适配器，基于Google的qsim实现OpenMP、AVX和CUDA后端，并引入近似QFT（AQFT）截断小角度受控旋转。

Result: 在AMD EPYC Zen2上AVX性能与多线程FFTW相当（64线程）；在NVIDIA A100上CUDA后端相比AVX和FFTW在较大规模问题上速度提升超过4倍；AQFT在保持精度的同时降低了电路深度和运行时间。

Conclusion: QFT→FFT证明了在经典模拟器上执行QFT电路可以成为传统FFT的高性能替代方案，尤其在GPU后端展现出显著优势，而AQFT进一步提供了精度-效率的权衡选项。

Abstract: We introduce QFT$\rightarrow$FFT, a family of HPC FFT libraries that compute the discrete Fourier transform by executing a quantum Fourier transform (QFT) circuit on classical quantum computer simulators. Input arrays are mapped directly to state amplitudes with explicit normalization/indexing, making QFT a drop-in replacement for FFT primitives. A backend-agnostic planner builds a fused-gate schedule and memory layout adapters to increase arithmetic intensity and reduce memory data movement. We implement this design on top of Google's C++ \texttt{qsim} and evaluate OpenMP, AVX, and CUDA backends. On an AMD EPYC Zen2 processor, our AVX performance is on par with that of multithreaded FFTW, utilizing 64 threads. On an NVIDIA A100, the CUDA backend achieves more than $4\times$ lower time than both AVX and FFTW on AMD EPYC Zen2 at larger sizes. We also employ an approximate QFT (AQFT) that truncates small-angle controlled rotations beyond a cutoff $k$, reducing circuit depth and runtime while preserving accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Conflict-Aware Retriever Editing for Knowledge Injection Attacks on LLM-Based RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18310)
*Xinru Liu,Xianglong Zhang,Di Cai,Zhumin Chen,Pengfei Hu,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAREATTACK，一种面向RAG系统的模型中心检索器攻击框架，通过冲突感知的检索器参数编辑和攻击保持的锚点修复，实现恶意知识注入。实验表明该方法能有效提升恶意段落被检索到的概率，揭示了开源检索模型带来的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG注入攻击多依赖于数据层面的恶意语料构造，生成的合成文本可被检测导致攻击失败。随着开源检索模型的普及，模型中心攻击面暴露，亟需探索通过直接编辑检索模型参数来实现更隐蔽的恶意知识注入方法。

Method: CAREATTACK包含两个阶段：(1) 冲突感知检索器编辑：将高效闭式参数编辑适配到稠密检索模型，通过图基冲突检测和参数编辑投影，促进恶意知识在检索中超越良性竞争段落，同时解决参数冲突；(2) 攻击保持锚点修复：对编辑后的检索器进行轻量校准，消除对非目标提示的影响，同时保持对目标提示的攻击有效性。

Result: 在Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B和BGE-M3上对三个基准数据集进行实验，结果表明该方法能显著提升恶意段落被RAG系统检索到的概率，并能对批量目标提示和段落执行攻击。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了基于开源检索模型构建的RAG系统存在一个实用的攻击面，即通过模型中心的方式编辑检索器参数即可实现恶意知识注入，对AI应用安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Injecting malicious knowledge into retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can manipulate retrieved evidence and mislead downstream generation, posing a serious security threat for AI applications. Existing RAG injection attacks mainly rely on manipulating external knowledge bases, such as crafting malicious corpus. However, the synthetic text crafted by such data-centric methods could be detectable, leading to the failure of attacks. Beyond corpus manipulation, open-source retrievers are increasingly exposing RAG systems to model-centric attacks. In this paper, we propose conflict-aware retriever editing, i.e., CAREATTACK, a model-centric retriever attack framework for malicious knowledge injection in RAG. Specifically, CAREATTACK consists two stages of conflict-aware retriever editing and attack-preserving anchor repair. Conflict-aware retriever editing adapts efficient closed-form parameter editing to the dense retrieval model, promoting malicious knowledge above benign competing passages and resolving potential parameter conflicts through graph-based conflict detection and parameter editing projection. Then, attack-preserving anchor repair performs lightweight calibration on the edited retriever to further eliminate the impact on non-target prompts while preserving the attack effectiveness for target prompts. We instantiate CAREATTACK on Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B and BGE-M3, and conduct evaluation on three benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate our method substantially promote malicious passages into the retrieved knowledge of RAG systems and can perform attacks for batches of target prompts and passages, given the access of retrieval model parameters. Since most RAG systems are built upon open-source retrieval models, this work reveals a practical attack surface in RAG systems. Codes are public accessible at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CareAttack-3F1C.

</details>


### [163] [TIGER: Inverting Transformer Gradients via Embedding-Subspace Distance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18312)
*William Kalikman,Ivo Petrov,Dimitar I. Dimitrov,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TIGER是一种针对Transformer模型的连续梯度反转攻击方法，通过将子空间信号转化为可微目标，直接优化词元嵌入以最小化到子空间的距离，在编码器模型上显著提升重建质量与速度，在解码器模型上比现有方法更鲁棒，首次在差分隐私保护的联邦学习场景中实现成功重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Transformer的梯度反转攻击存在两大局限：一是基于优化虚拟输入匹配梯度更新的方法代价高且不稳定；二是利用注意力梯度低秩性质的子空间攻击在数值噪声（如量化或差分隐私）下脆弱，且对于非因果注意力的编码器模型扩展性差。

Method: TIGER将子空间信号转化为可微的目标函数，不进行离散词元搜索或全梯度匹配，而是直接优化词元嵌入，使其到子空间的距离最小化。

Result: 在编码器模型上，TIGER显著提升了重建质量和运行速度；在解码器模型上，TIGER比现有子空间攻击更鲁棒，首次在差分隐私保护的联邦学习场景中实现了成功重建。

Conclusion: TIGER通过连续优化方法克服了现有离散搜索和子空间攻击的局限性，在存在数值噪声（如DP）的场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的梯度反转攻击。

Abstract: Federated learning allows multiple clients to jointly train a shared model by sending gradient updates to a central server while keeping raw inputs local. However, prior gradient inversion attacks show that these updates can reveal enough information to reconstruct client inputs. Existing attacks on transformers either optimize dummy inputs to match the true client updates, which is costly and unstable for modern models, or exploit the low rank of attention gradients to identify a subspace containing the true layer embeddings, followed by a discrete membership test for candidate tokens. However, this token test is brittle under numerical noise, i.e., from quantization or Differential Privacy (DP), and scales poorly for encoder models with non-causal attention. We introduce TIGER, a continuous gradient inversion attack that turns this subspace signal into a differentiable objective. Instead of searching over tokens or matching full gradients, TIGER directly optimizes token embeddings to minimize their distance to the subspace. Our experiments demonstrate that on encoder-only models, TIGER substantially improves both reconstruction quality and runtime over existing attacks, while on decoder models, TIGER is more robust than prior subspace-based attacks, enabling the first successful reconstructions in DP-defended federated learning settings.

</details>


### [164] [TopVenues: A Reproducible Corpus and Tooling Substrate for Cybersecurity Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18320)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TopVenues 是一个开源系统，将网络安全文献综述中的语料库构建过程转化为版本化的、可审计的研究工件。它基于 DBLP 元数据，通过开放 API 和出版商提取器丰富论文记录（摘要和 BibTeX 覆盖率达 99.9% 以上），存储在单调 SQLite 快照中，支持 CLI、Web 界面和导出。该系统固定了文献综述的“分母”，并实现了可重复的测量：发现 2024-2025 年顶级安全会议论文中 29.2% 有 arXiv 预印本，中位提前 5 个月；基于作者历史记录的过滤器在 90% 召回率下实现 16.5 倍精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全文献综述需要一个可复现的“分母”（即筛选前纳入的论文集合），但现有方法依赖出版商门户、书目索引和学术 API，这些工具的覆盖范围、格式和查询语义随时间变化，导致综述不可复现、不可审计。

Method: TopVenues 系统：声明会议/期刊和年份范围，以 DBLP 计算机科学书目数据库为元数据主干，通过开放学术 API 和出版商特定提取器丰富论文的摘要和 BibTeX 条目，将结果存储在单调的 SQLite 快照中，提供 CLI、Web 界面和导出路径。

Result: 2026年5月快照包含来自 11 个网络安全来源的 9,925 篇论文（2017-2026年），摘要覆盖率达 99.86%，BibTeX 覆盖率达 99.99%。全文关键词搜索在 31 毫秒内完成，250 项测试验证了数据完整性。29.2% 的 2024-2025 年顶级安全会议论文以 arXiv 预印本形式出现，中位提前 5 个月；作者历史记录过滤器在 90% 召回率下实现 16.5 倍精度提升。

Conclusion: TopVenues 通过将语料库本身变为可执行、可检查、可引用的工件，将语料库构建与可审计的网络安全测量联系起来，使文献综述可复现，并支持可重复的元科学测量。

Abstract: Cybersecurity literature reviews require a reproducible denominator: the set of papers that a protocol includes before screening and synthesis begin. Today, that denominator is often reconstructed from publisher portals, bibliographic indices, and scholarly application programming interfaces (APIs) whose coverage, formats, and query semantics change over time. This paper presents TopVenues, an open-source system that materializes corpus construction as a versioned research artifact. TopVenues declares a venue and year scope, uses DBLP Computer Science Bibliography (DBLP) as the metadata spine, enriches records with abstracts and BibTeX entries via open scholarly APIs and publisher-specific extractors, and stores the results in a monotonic SQLite snapshot, accessible via a command-line interface (CLI), a web interface, and export paths for review workflows. The May 2026 snapshot contains 9,925 papers from 11 cybersecurity sources over 2017 to 2026, with 99.86% abstract coverage and 99.99% BibTeX coverage; keyword search over the full corpus completes in under 31 ms, and a 250-test suite validates the data-integrity invariants. The fixed denominator also enables repeatable measurement: 29.2% of 2024 to 2025 papers from the four top-ranked security conferences in our scope appear as arXiv preprints, with a median of five months before publication, and a prior-author-track-record filter yields a 16.5x precision gain at 90% recall for triaging preprints that later appear in the same venue set. TopVenues links corpus construction to auditable cybersecurity measurement by making the corpus itself executable, inspectable, and citable. The artifact is available at https://github.com/sidneibarbieri/topVenues.

</details>


### [165] [QSignAI: Quantum-Randomness-Seeded Identity Signatures at the Intersection of AI for Science and Science for AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27729)
*Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了QSignAI平台，该平台在实时事件参与系统中实现了人工智能与量子计算的单向结合——通过量子随机性生成（基于双源提取器和单量子比特Hadamard测量）为AI驱动的社交平台提供唯一身份签名，并利用AI聊天机器人使量子现象对普通观众可理解。系统已成功部署，但目前使用云端量子模拟器，物理QPU是未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管2024-2025年诺贝尔奖和图灵奖同时认可了AI和量子科学，但尚无已部署的系统将这两者结合起来为公众服务。

Method: 构建了一个对话机器人，将每位参与者的第一条消息路由通过量子流水线：该流水线包含在SV1和DM1模拟器上基于独立单量子比特Hadamard测量的Toeplitz双源提取器，以及一个2量子比特Bell态，为每位参与者生成独特的量子随机性种子身份签名。

Result: 前两个问题通过系统架构和来自实时活动的定性部署证据得到回答；第三个问题通过成功的生产部署得到验证。当前部署使用云端量子模拟器，物理QPU随机性被确定为近期扩展方向。可量化基准测试被列为优先未来工作。

Conclusion: QSignAI证明了AI与量子计算在已部署系统中的可行结合，为公众提供了可感知的量子现象体验，但当前依赖模拟器而非真实量子硬件，且缺乏量化评估指标。

Abstract: The 2024-2025 Nobel and Turing awards recognised AI and quantum science simultaneously. Yet no deployed system has brought these streams together for the public. This paper presents QSignAI, a production-deployed platform demonstrating a bidirectional AI-quantum relationship in a real-time event participation system. We address three questions: can quantum-randomness generation via a two-source extractor be embedded in an AI-driven social platform with acceptable latency; can an AI bot make quantum phenomena perceptually legible to general audiences; and does the combined system work in practice? A conversational bot routes each participant's first message through a quantum pipeline comprising a Toeplitz two-source extractor over independent single-qubit Hadamard measurements on SV1 and DM1 simulators, plus a 2-qubit Bell state, producing a unique quantum-randomness-seeded identity signature per participant. The first two questions are answered through system architecture and qualitative deployment evidence from live events; the third through successful production deployment. The current deployment uses cloud quantum simulators; physical QPU randomness is the near-term extension. Measurable benchmarks are identified as priority future work.

</details>


### [166] [Agentra: A Supervisable Multi-Agent Framework for Enterprise Intrusion Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18325)
*Raj Patel,Shaswata Mitra,Michele Guida,Stefano Iannucci,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Agentra是一个可监督的多智能体入侵响应系统框架，通过角色分离的智能体将告警转化为结构化的响应计划，在120事件语料库上将FP-aware IRS F1从0.61提升至0.84，并将有害动作率恢复至0.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业入侵响应仍依赖静态剧本和分析师手动分类，造成告警生成与响应阻断之间存在延迟，亟需自动化且可审计的响应方案。

Method: 提出Agentra框架，包含角色分离的多智能体（Planner-Validator审查循环、Moderator安全网关、Action Catalog风险评分）、基于MITRE ATT&CK/D3FEND/NIST CSF 2.0的结构化响应计划生成、以及追加审计日志。

Result: 在ThreatHunter-Playbook、Splunk BOTSv3和DARPA OpTC组成的120事件语料上，最强配置将FP-aware IRS F1从0.61提升至0.84，并将有害动作率从Planner-only配置的不可控水平恢复至0.0%。

Conclusion: 多智能体响应规划能够在保持分析师审批与可审计性的前提下，提升基于本体的IRS覆盖率，有效抑制过度反应风险。

Abstract: Enterprise intrusion response still depends on static playbooks and analyst-driven triage, creating delay between alert generation and containment. We present Agentra, a supervisable multi-agent Intrusion Response System (IRS) framework that converts alerts from IDS, EDR, and XDR platforms into structured incident response plans grounded in MITRE ATT&CK, MITRE D3FEND, and NIST CSF 2.0. Agentra decomposes response reasoning across role-scoped agents, validates proposed plans through a bounded Planner--Validator review loop, screens retrieved threat intelligence through a Moderator security gateway, gates actions through an Action Catalog and risk score, and records decisions in an append-only audit log. We evaluate Agentra against a static OASIS CACAO v2.0 cyber-playbook baseline on a 120-event corpus drawn from ThreatHunter-Playbook, Splunk BOTSv3, and DARPA OpTC. The strongest configuration improves FP-aware IRS F1 from 0.61 to 0.84 and restores the projected harmful-action rate to the static baseline level of 0.0% after Planner-only configurations introduce unsafe overreaction. These results indicate that multi-agent response planning can improve ontology-grounded IRS coverage while preserving analyst approval and auditability.

</details>


### [167] [SafeClawBench: Separating Semantic, Audit-Evidence, and Sandbox Harm in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18356)
*Yuchuan Tian,Mengyu Zheng,Haocheng Mei,Ye Yuan,Chao Xu,Xinghao Chen,Hanting Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeClawBench 是一个面向工具使用型语言模型智能体的分阶段安全基准测试，包含600个对抗性任务和三个独立评估端点（语义接受度、审计可见危害、沙箱工具/状态危害），揭示了不同模型和提示策略下的安全失败模式，并发现语义检查通过后仍可能产生实际可执行的危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将工具型智能体的安全失败不同阶段（如同意攻击与产生实际危害）合并为单一的攻击成功率指标，难以区分模型仅口头同意攻击者还是产生了可观察的实际损害，因此需要一种分阶段的细粒度安全评估方法。

Method: 构建了包含600个对抗性任务的SafeClawBench基准，覆盖六类攻击（直接/间接提示注入、工具返回注入、记忆投毒、记忆提取、歧义驱动不安全推理），设置三个独立评估端点：语义攻击接受度、审计可见危害证据、沙箱观察的工具/状态危害。在五种智能体端点和四种提示级别策略下进行评估。

Result: 语义失败率在不同模型间差异显著（9.0%-44.2%）；审计可见危害范围窄于语义失败；在12,000行的匹配分析中，347次沙箱危害中有291次发生在通过语义检查的行中，表明语义合规并不保证无实际执行危害；提示策略会改变端点结果，但效果取决于模型和协议。

Conclusion: SafeClawBench提供了一个可复现的框架，用于比较智能体模型和提示策略条件，避免将文本合规、证据支持的危害与可执行状态变化混为一谈，从而更准确地评估工具使用型智能体的实际安全风险。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents introduce security failures that go beyond unsafe text: they can disclose protected objects, write persistent memory, send messages, modify databases, or trigger harmful code and tool effects. Existing evaluations often collapse these stages into a single attack success rate, making it difficult to tell whether a model merely agreed with an attacker or actually produced observable harm. We introduce SafeClawBench, a staged benchmark for tool-using agent security with 600 controlled adversarial tasks across six attack families: direct and indirect prompt injection, tool-return injection, memory poisoning, memory extraction, and ambiguity-driven unsafe inference. SafeClawBench reports three separate endpoints: semantic attack acceptance, audit-visible harm evidence, and sandbox-observed tool/state harm. Evaluating five agent endpoints under four prompt-level policies, we find that these endpoints capture different failure modes. Without additional prompt protection, semantic failure rates vary widely across models, from 9.0% to 44.2%. Audited harm evidence is narrower than semantic failure, and under a separate executable protocol some matched task identities produce sandbox harm despite passing the Semantic Core call: in a 12,000-row matched analysis, 291 of 347 observed sandbox harms occur in rows that pass the semantic check. Prompt policies change endpoint outcomes, but their effects depend on both model and protocol. SafeClawBench provides a reproducible framework for comparing agent models and prompt-policy conditions without conflating textual compliance, evidence-supported harm, and executable state changes. The open-source dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/sairights/safeclawbench.

</details>


### [168] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of LLMs in Aiding Compliance Testing of PKCS#1-v1.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18405)
*Polina Kozyreva,Endadul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了将语法级变异与LLM代码合成相结合的方法，用于二进制协议规范合规性测试。在PKCS#1 v1.5签名验证标准上，该方法复现了Morpheus识别出的13个非平凡违规类别中的10个（包括全部5个签名伪造类别），并发现1个此前未报告的不一致。但LLM幻觉是限制效果的主要因素，82.5%的生成脚本存在幻觉，结构变异忠实度仅13.3%，约束变异正确率30.3%但变异被忽略率达8.1%。尽管操作可靠性达99.8%，语义忠实度仅17.5%，表明两者存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制协议规范合规性测试中，纯随机生成和原始变异难以探索依赖TLV编码的协议的有意义行为，而领域特定测试工具需要深厚的协议专业知识和大量人工构建成本。本工作旨在探索语法级变异与LLM代码合成相结合是否能成为一种更通用且可行的规范合规性测试方法。

Method: 将语法级变异与LLM代码合成相结合，在PKCS#1 v1.5签名验证标准上对48个密码学库实现进行测试。使用Morpheus作为形式化验证测试预言机，评估方法对规范违规的检测能力，并系统分析LLM幻觉的类型与分布。

Result: 复现了Morpheus识别的13个非平凡违规类别中的10个（包括全部5个签名伪造类别），发现1个此前未报告的不一致。但82.5%的生成脚本存在LLM幻觉，结构变异忠实度仅13.3%，约束变异正确率30.3%但变异被忽略率达8.1%。操作可靠性99.8%与语义忠实度17.5%之间存在显著差距。识别出五种不同的幻觉类型，其分布在变异类别间系统性地变化。

Conclusion: 语法级变异+LLM代码合成在规范合规性测试中有一定潜力（能复现多数违规类别并发现新不一致），但LLM幻觉是主要瓶颈。操作可靠性与语义忠实度之间的巨大差距提供了关于LLM生成代码何时可在规范驱动测试中可信使用的行动指南。未来需要解决幻觉问题以提升方法的有效性。

Abstract: Testing implementations of binary protocols for specification compliance requires inputs that satisfy both structural and semantic constraints. Purely random generation and primitive mutations are often insufficient for exploring semantically meaningful behaviors in protocols that rely on Type-Length-Value (TLV) encoding, yet domain-specific compliance testing tools require deep protocol expertise and significant manual effort to construct. This work investigates whether grammar-level mutation combined with LLM-based code synthesis can serve as a viable, more generalizable approach to specification compliance testing.
  We evaluate the approach on PKCS#1 v1.5 signature verification -- a widely deployed TLV-encoded standard with a formally verified testing oracle (Morpheus) -- across 48 cryptographic library implementations. We reproduced 10 of 13 non-trivial specification violation categories previously identified by Morpheus, including all 5 signature forgery categories, and discovered 1 previously unreported discrepancy. We found that LLM hallucination -- occurring in 82.5% of generated scripts -- is the primary factor limiting effectiveness, not the mutation strategies. We identify five distinct hallucination types and show that their distribution varies systematically across mutation categories: structural mutations are implemented with 13.3% fidelity while constraint mutations achieve 30.3% correctness but suffer the highest rate of mutations being fully ignored (8.1%). These findings reveal a striking gap between operational reliability (99.8%) and semantic fidelity (17.5%), providing actionable guidance on when LLM-based code synthesis can be trusted in specification-driven testing pipelines.

</details>


### [169] [Understanding the "Airport" Censorship Circumvention Ecosystem in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18427)
*Rumaisa Habib,Mingshi Wu,Shiva Shahandeh,Min Ni,Eric Wustrow,Zakir Durumeric*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了中国“机场”订阅制翻墙代理生态系统，发现其是中国最流行的现成翻墙工具，超过一半的受访者使用；通过扫描和订阅识别出3,431个活跃机场，性能常优于直连，但存在支付风险、政府打击和自身审查政策等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中国地下市场出现了一种名为“机场”的订阅制翻墙代理服务，但学术界对此缺乏系统性研究，其规模、技术特性、使用模式和风险尚不清楚。

Method: 结合用户调查（1,667份问卷）、社交媒体分析、主动网络测量，通过扫描互联网和抓取Telegram公告频道识别机场，并订阅35个机场进行性能表征。

Result: 发现“机场”是现阶段中国最流行的现成翻墙工具，超过一半受访者使用；识别出3,431个活跃机场；其性能常超越直连GFW，采用多跳架构；但存在商业支付风险、频繁被政府打击、客户端配置困难及自身部署审查政策等问题。

Conclusion: “机场”的使用远较学术文献中其他翻墙工具广泛，但引入了新的脆弱性和控制形式，为未来翻墙研究提供了经验与机遇。

Abstract: In China, a burgeoning underground market sells citizens subscription-based censorship circumvention proxies known as ''airports''. We present the first systematic study of this ecosystem, combining user surveys, social media analysis, and active network measurements. We find that airports are by far the most popular off-the-shelf censorship circumvention tool in China, used by over half of our 1,667~survey respondents, who cite their ease of use, performance, and access to geo-restricted services like ChatGPT and Netflix. By scanning the Internet and scraping Telegram announcement channels, we identify 3,431 active airports built on a handful of open-source toolkits. We subscribe to 35 airports and characterize their performance, which often surpasses direct connections through the Great Firewall due to a distinctive multi-hop architecture. However, airports also pose new challenges and security risks: they accept payment through commercial services like Alipay, suffer frequent government takedowns, and are difficult for clients to configure optimally. Many airports also deploy their own distinct censorship policies. Airports are far more widely used than other circumvention tools from the academic literature, but introduce new forms of fragility and control, offering both lessons and opportunities for future circumvention research.

</details>


### [170] [Ghost Vectors: Soft-Deleted Embeddings Remain Reconstructible in HNSW Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18497)
*Chandranil Chakraborttii,Jackeline García Alvarado,Sitora Abdulofizova,Shivanshu Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现代RAG系统中HNSW向量数据库的软删除漏洞：物理删除的向量仍可从原始索引文件中恢复，并成功从多种数据类型中重建敏感信息（如人名、地理位置、病历标记、人脸身份）。作者提出Epoch Key Rotation方法，通过加密向量并在删除时丢弃密钥，将PII恢复率降至0%，同时生成可审计的删除证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在处理数据删除请求时仅执行软删除（标记删除但物理保留嵌入向量），这违反了GDPR第17条（被遗忘权）和HIPAA等数据保护法规。需要验证该漏洞的实际风险并提出合规的解决方案。

Method: 1) 对三种HNSW实现的原始索引文件进行存储层分析，验证软删除向量的物理可恢复性；2) 使用Vec2Text逆映射模型（无领域微调）在不同数据集和模态上测试恢复效果；3) 提出Epoch Key Rotation：对向量进行加密，删除时丢弃加密密钥，并生成ECDSA签名的加密证明记录删除事件。

Result: 软删除漏洞下：Wikipedia BLP数据集恢复25.5%精确人名和46.4%地理位置；NIH Synthea数据集恢复100%患者年龄和性别标记；组织病理学图像嵌入100%分类准确率；人脸嵌入Top-1身份恢复达99%。Epoch Key Rotation将PII恢复率降至0%，处理500条删除向量仅需2.5ms（约0.005ms/条）。

Conclusion: HNSW向量数据库的软删除机制存在严重隐私泄露风险，可通过存储层物理访问恢复被"删除"的敏感数据。Epoch Key Rotation提供了轻量级、可审计的合规删除方案，但需要在实际系统中进一步集成验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) allows large language models to access external and private corpora for factual, domain-specific responses. Modern RAG pipelines use hierarchical navigable small world (HNSW) vector databases for efficient similarity search. When a user requests data deletion, the systems typically only mark the record as deleted, leaving the embedding on disk physically unchanged. This soft-delete operation raises compliance concerns under data-erasure and retention requirements such as GDPR Article 17 and HIPAA. Analysis on three HNSW implementations confirms that deleted vectors remain physically recoverable by accessing the raw index files at the storage layer, bypassing API access. Using the Vec2Text inversion model without domain-specific fine-tuning, we show this vulnerability on multiple real-world datasets and data modalities. On Wikipedia biographical living persons dataset (BLP), we successfully recover 25.5% of exact person names and 46.4% of geographic locations (ROUGE-L 0.185). Recovery reaches 100% for both patient age and gender markers (ROUGE-L 0.290) on highly structured, sensitive data (NIH Synthea dataset). On soft-deleted image embeddings, we show 100% tissue classification on histopathology patches (p=1.02e-07) and top-1 identity recovery reaches 99% on facial embeddings (p<0.01). This work introduces Epoch Key Rotation, which encrypts vectors and discards the key upon deletion. Epoch key rotation reduces observed PII recovery to 0% and completes in 2.5 ms for 500 deleted vectors (approximately 0.005 ms/record). Additionally, it generates an ECDSA-signed cryptographic proof as an auditable record of the deletion event.

</details>


### [171] [From Bits to Mixed-Radix Keys: Horner Decomposition, Uniform Sampling, and the Information-Theoretic QKD Interface of the MR-OTP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18526)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了混合基一次性密码本（MR-OTP），将经典OTP扩展到异构字母表，并通过霍纳方法及其逆映射实现了从QKD二进制熵到均匀混合基密钥的无偏转换，证明了拒绝采样的最优性，建立了端到端信息论安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典一次性密码本（OTP）从同质二进制字母表扩展到异构（混合基数）字母表，以适配不同字符集的实际应用场景，同时保持完美保密性。

Method: 1) 利用霍纳方法（Horner's method）及其逆映射作为二进制整数与混合基元组之间的自然映射；2) 采用拒绝采样（rejection sampling）消除朴素模约化引入的偏差；3) 构建批处理提取器（batched extractor）。

Result: 1) 证明朴素模约化会引入偏差；2) 证明拒绝采样能以最优期望代价恢复均匀性；3) 建立了单会话和多会话管道的端到端信息论安全性；4) 量化了效率增益；5) 给出了基恢复问题（Base Recovery Problem）的无条件和条件结果。

Conclusion: MR-OTP在保持完美保密性的前提下，为异构字母表提供了实用的密钥生成和安全通信方案，结合QKD源实现了可证明安全的加密管道。

Abstract: The Mixed-Radix One-Time Pad (MR-OTP) extends the classical OTP to heterogeneous alphabets while preserving perfect secrecy. We provide a practical, bias-free method to convert raw binary entropy from a QKD source into uniform mixed-radix keys by identifying Horner's method and its inverse as the natural mapping between binary integers and mixed-radix tuples. We show that naive modular reduction induces bias and prove that rejection sampling restores uniformity with optimal expected cost. We establish end-to-end information-theoretic security for single and multi-session pipelines, quantify efficiency gains, present a batched extractor, and give unconditional and conditional results on the Base Recovery Problem.

</details>


### [172] [Evaluating Prompting-Based Defenses Against Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18530)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种基于提示的防御策略（重点标注、释义、提示三明治及其组合）对抗领域伪装注入攻击的效果。在3,510次试验中，跨三个模型家族（Claude Haiku, Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash）和三个部署领域（金融、法律、通用）的实验表明：释义检索内容是最一致的防御策略，可将伪装攻击成功率降低55-84%；防御效果高度依赖模型；金融领域面临最高残余风险（26-33%基线成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域伪装注入攻击使用领域特定词汇嵌入恶意指令，能够规避依赖句法注入标记的传统检测器。当检测失败时，从业人员需要知道哪些防御架构能有效降低攻击成功率，但此前缺乏针对这类伪装类注入攻击的提示防御系统性评估。

Method: 采用3,510次试验，跨三个模型家族（Claude Haiku, Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash）和三个部署领域（金融、法律、通用），评估五种提示防御策略（重点标注、释义、提示三明治及两种组合）对抗领域伪装注入攻击的效果。

Result: 释义检索内容是最有效的防御策略，将伪装攻击成功率降低55-84%；防御效果高度依赖模型，重点标注在Claude Haiku上将攻击成功率减半，但对Llama 3.1 8B无效；金融领域残余风险最高（26-33%基线成功率），无提示防御能完全消除弱模型的威胁。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个针对伪装类注入攻击的提示防御系统性评估，建立了基于基准的实践者推荐，但所有任务使用合成构建的专业文档，这些基准排名能否泛化到真实企业文档仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Domain-camouflaged injection attacks embed malicious instructions in retrieved content using domain-appropriate vocabulary, evading standard detectors that rely on syntactic injection markers. When detection fails, practitioners need to know which defense architectures reduce attack success. We evaluate five prompting-based defenses (spotlighting, paraphrasing, prompt sandwiching, and two combinations) against domain-camouflaged injection across three model families (Claude Haiku, Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash) and three deployment domains (financial, legal, general) using 3,510 trials. Paraphrasing retrieved content before agent processing is the most consistently effective defense in this benchmark, reducing camouflage attack success rate by 55-84\% depending on model, and achieves lower attack success rates than our Llama Guard 4 configuration on every model tested. Defense effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: spotlighting halves attack success on Claude Haiku but provides no benefit on Llama 3.1 8B. Financial domain deployments face the highest residual risk at 26-33\% baseline attack success rate, with no prompting-based defense fully eliminating the threat on weaker models. These results provide the first systematic evaluation of prompting-based defenses specifically against camouflage-class injection attacks and establish benchmark-based recommendations for practitioners. All tasks use synthetically constructed professional documents; whether these benchmark rankings generalize to real enterprise documents remains an open question.

</details>


### [173] [AI Sandboxes: A Threat Model, Taxonomy, and Measurement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18532)
*Inderjeet Singh,Haitham Mahmoud,Andrés Murillo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地构建了AI沙盒的保障性分析框架，形式化定义了沙盒边界与最弱链路规则，区分了主要沙盒原型，建立了包含对保障装置本身攻击的网络-物理威胁模型，以及涵盖保真度、可控性、可观测性、包容性、可复现性和治理工件六个维度的测量框架，并通过三个真实案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估日益依赖受限的沙盒环境，但对于物理AI、AIoT和网络-物理系统，沙盒的测试边界、保障证据的合成规则以及威胁模型缺乏系统化的形式化描述，亟需建立统一的保障性理论框架以明确沙盒能有效测试什么、能包容哪些风险、以及能支持何种形式的证据。

Method: 本文采用形式化分析方法，定义了沙盒边界(B)及按维度证据合成部署声明的最弱链路规则；划分了主要沙盒原型；建立了包含对保障装置本身攻击(如传感器欺骗、通信干扰、监管逃避)的网络-物理威胁模型；设计了涵盖六个维度的测量框架；并在三个真实沙盒案例上进行了实例化应用。

Result: 提出了沙盒边界的形式化定义和证据合成的最弱链路规则；区分了数字AI、具身自主和网络-物理部署下的主要沙盒原型；建立了包含攻击保障装置本身的网络-物理威胁模型；开发了六维测量框架；通过三个案例验证了框架的可操作性和解释力。

Conclusion: 该威胁模型、分类体系和测量框架为AI沙盒的有效性评估提供了理论基础，明确了沙盒在不同场景下能有效测试的范围、能包容的风险类型以及能为安全、安保和监管保障提供的证据形式。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly evaluated in bounded environments that combine isolation, simulation, instrumentation, supervision, and evidence capture. For physical AI, AIoT, and cyber-physical systems, this shift is not a matter of terminology: the system under test may sense, decide, actuate, communicate, and fail through physical processes, networked devices, and human operators. This article develops an assurance-oriented account of AI sandboxes as controlled environments for testing, evaluation, verification, and validation across digital AI, embodied autonomy, and cyber-physical deployments. We formalize the sandbox boundary and a weakest-link rule for composing per-dimension evidence into a bounded deployment claim; separate major sandbox archetypes; define a cyber-physical threat model that includes attacks on the assurance apparatus itself; and introduce a measurement framework spanning fidelity, controllability, observability, containment, reproducibility, and governance artifacts, instantiated on three worked case studies of real sandboxes. The resulting threat model, taxonomy, and measurement framework clarify what a sandbox can validly test, which risks it can contain, and what forms of evidence it can support for safety, security, and regulatory assurance.

</details>


### [174] [Confident yet Concerned: Inconsistencies in Computing Students' Attitudes on Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18541)
*Victor Adama,Robert Biddle,Nalin Arachchilage,Danielle Lottridge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对236名计算机专业大学生的调查，发现尽管他们对网络安全有基本认识，但存在错误信念、课堂外学习、受害经历以及自信与担忧并存等矛盾，并通过聚类分析识别出两类不同态度群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人（尤其是技术娴熟的年轻成年人）在管理在线隐私和应对网络钓鱼攻击方面感到无力，但其对网络安全的整体态度和管理方式尚不明确，需要深入研究。

Method: 对236名计算机专业大学生（被视为"领先用户"）进行问卷调查，结合开放式回答的主题分析与定量数据，并通过聚类分析识别态度群体。

Result: 学生展现出合理的网络安全意识，但经历差异大，存在实践、责任认知和支持结构方面的不一致。发现四个关键主题张力：知识丰富但有错误信念、课堂外学习、受害经历有限支持、自信与担忧并存。聚类分析识别出两类群体，其中一类感到准备不足、缺乏自信但渴望学习。自我效能感与自信相关并能预测聚类归属。

Conclusion: 年轻技术用户对网络安全的态度和实践存在内在矛盾和群体异质性，需要针对不同群体（尤其是缺乏自信但渴望学习者）提供差异化支持和教育干预。

Abstract: Today's young adults are most immersed in technology, leading in feelings of powerlessness in managing online privacy across many platforms, and particularly susceptible to phishing attacks. This raises questions about their general, wide-ranging attitudes towards and management of cybersecurity. How do young, tech-savvy adults approach cybersecurity? We seek a better understanding of their cybersecurity knowledge, attitudes and experiences, in particular in addressing deceptive online communications. We surveyed a group of `lead users': computing university students (n = 236). By combining thematic analysis of open-ended responses with quantitative data, we provide insights into their experiences and perceptions. While students demonstrate reasonable cybersecurity awareness, their cybersecurity experiences vary, and inconsistencies exist around their practices, perceptions of responsibility, and support structures. Findings also reveal four key thematic tensions: 1) Computing students are knowledgeable yet have persistent incorrect beliefs, 2) They learn more about keeping safe from sources outside the classroom, 3) They have limited assistance and have fallen victim to cybercrime, and 4) Many are confident, yet others are concerned about their own safety and responsibility. Through cluster analysis of attitudes, we identify two groups, with one feeling less prepared, less confident, yet expressing a desire to learn more. Established measures of intentions and objective knowledge were correlated to preparedness. Self-efficacy correlated to confidence and predicted cluster membership.

</details>


### [175] [The Gate Is Only as Honest as Its Contracts: ContractGuard for the Contract Layer of Risk-Aware Causal Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18550)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）框架，通过从智能体可见动作空间中移除危险工具来防御间接提示注入攻击，并引入ContractGuard验证器在受控基准上将注入成功率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型LLM智能体面临间接提示注入攻击威胁，现有防御机制存在信任假设盲区，需要更根本的结构性安全保障。

Method: 提出RACG框架：通过因果门控先于准入门控，在工具注册表与门控之间引入ContractGuard验证层，实现签名来源验证、类型化合约认证和运行时效果验证。

Result: 在受控基准测试中，ContractGuard对所有建模攻击（包括穷尽白盒自适应攻击）将注入成功率恢复至零，且不会过度拒绝诚实合约；在六种当前代托管模型上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 工具合约的效果完整性（而非风险标签）是安全的核心承载假设，RACG的结构性保证依赖于合约本身的完整性；ContractGuard有效弥补了这一信任缺口。

Abstract: Risk-Aware Causal Gating (RACG) defends tool-augmented LLM agents against indirect prompt injection by removing dangerous tools from the agent's visible action space, so that even a fully injection-compliant agent cannot call a tool it cannot see. We make three points. First, this structural guarantee does not eliminate the trust assumption behind safe tool use; it relocates it into the integrity of the tool contracts -- declared preconditions, effects, risk, and authorization -- that the gate reads, so an attacker who corrupts a contract can make the gate mis-decide without ever persuading the agent. Second, forging a tool's effects is strictly more dangerous than tampering with its risk label, because RACG applies a causal gate before its admissibility gate: an off-path tool is never exposed, so risk-relabeling alone fails, whereas effect forgery routes the dangerous tool onto the causal path and succeeds. Effect integrity, not the risk label, is the load-bearing assumption. Third, we introduce ContractGuard, a verifier between the registry and the gate that layers signed provenance, typed contract attestation, and runtime effect verification; on a controlled benchmark it restores injection success to zero against every modeled attack -- including an exhaustive white-box adaptive attacker -- without over-rejecting honest contracts, and the structural prediction is confirmed on six current-generation hosted models (Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 4.6, Haiku 4.5; Amazon Nova Premier and Nova 2 Lite; GPT-OSS-120B).

</details>


### [176] [MIDS: Detecting Stealthy Masquerade and Tampering Attacks on CAN Bus via Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18599)
*Qiqi Liu,Runhan Song,Lei Cui,Heng Zhang,Yuyan Sun,Limin Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对CAN总线缺乏加密认证导致的伪装攻击（masquerade）难题，本文提出MIDS双流入侵检测系统，利用双向选择性状态空间建模并行处理标识符和载荷，在特斯拉Model 3实车采集的超1亿帧数据及54种伪装攻击变体上达到96.94% F1，推理延迟仅1.147ms，并在四个公开基准上表现优于最强基线达13.94个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN总线入侵检测系统主要针对基于帧注入的构造型攻击（如DoS、模糊测试、ID欺骗），利用帧间到达统计等信号即可有效检测；但面对更难的伪装攻击——内部攻击者在原始发送时隙原位替换合法帧，保留流量周期性，使得基于流量统计的防御失效——现有方法难以应对。因此，需要针对伪装攻击场景设计新的检测方案。

Method: 提出Mamba入侵检测系统（MIDS），采用双流（dual-stream）框架并行处理CAN标识符（ID）和数据载荷（payload），通过双向选择性状态空间建模（bidirectional selective state-space modelling）重建二者的联合时序语义，从而捕获伪装攻击中ID与载荷之间的异常关联模式。

Result: 在特斯拉Model 3实车采集的超1亿帧数据（涵盖三种驾驶场景）上合成54种伪装攻击变体（ID-only、data-only、combined），MIDS达到96.94% F1，比最强可复现基线高出8个百分点以上；单窗口推理延迟仅1.147ms，满足实时车载部署要求。在ROAD、CrySyS、OTIDS、CT&T四个公开基准上（涵盖伪装和注入场景），MIDS达到93.70%-99.61% F1，在统一5折协议下比八个复现基线中最强者高出最多13.94个百分点。

Conclusion: MIDS通过双流双向状态空间建模有效解决了CAN总线伪装攻击检测难题，在实车数据和公开基准上均显著超越现有方法，且推理延迟极低，具备良好的实时部署可行性和泛化能力。

Abstract: The Controller Area Network (CAN) protocol is the primary communication standard for Electronic Control Units (ECUs) in modern vehicles, but its lack of encryption and authentication exposes it to a range of security threats. Existing intrusion detection systems are largely tuned to fabrication-style attacks (DoS, fuzzing, ID spoofing realised by frame injection), in which detection signals such as per-ID inter-arrival statistics are readily available. We instead address the harder \emph{masquerade} setting~\cite{b37}, in which an internal adversary substitutes a legitimate frame in-situ at its original transmission slot, preserving traffic periodicity and rendering traffic-statistic defences ineffective. We propose the Mamba Intrusion Detection System (MIDS), an innovative dual-stream framework that processes CAN identifiers and payloads in parallel and reconstructs their joint temporal semantics through bidirectional selective state-space modelling. To evaluate MIDS, we collected over 100 million CAN frames from a physical Tesla Model 3 across three driving regimes and synthesised 54 masquerade attack variants spanning ID-only, data-only, and combined modifications. MIDS attains an F1 of 96.94\% on this dataset, exceeding the strongest reproducible baseline by more than 8 percentage points, while sustaining a 1.147~ms single-window inference latency -- ample headroom for real-time onboard deployment. To verify generalisation, we further evaluate MIDS on four public benchmarks (ROAD, CrySyS, OTIDS, CT\&T) covering both masquerade and injection scenarios; MIDS attains F1 from 93.70\% to 99.61\%, outperforming the strongest of eight reproduced baselines by up to 13.94 percentage points under a unified 5-fold protocol.

</details>


### [177] [Code-Augur: Agentic Vulnerability Detection via Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18619)
*Zhengxiong Luo,Mehtab Zafar,Dylan Wolff,Abhik Roychoudhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Code-Augur，一种基于安全规范优先范式的智能体漏洞检测框架。它通过将智能体的隐性假设显式化为安全规范（代码断言），并利用引导式模糊测试持续运行时证伪这些规范，从而对齐智能体对代码意图的理解与代码实际行为。在真实项目中，Code-Augur比现有最先进智能体检测出更多漏洞，并在关键开源项目中发现了22个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体在漏洞检测中取得了突破性进展，但其推理过程极其不透明且未经验证。智能体对函数输入做出何种假设、推理失败和错误假设会导致漏洞漏报，降低对智能体分析的信任度。现有方法缺乏对智能体隐性假设的显式化与验证机制。

Method: 提出"安全规范优先"范式：(1) 将智能体的隐性假设显式化为安全规范（security specifications）；(2) 通过运行时证伪（runtime falsification）持续精炼这些规范。实现为Code-Augur框架：对代码库每个组件进行分析，将安全判断背后的局部不变式提交为源代码内断言；同时利用引导式模糊测试尝试证伪这些假设。当模糊测试触发断言时，要么发现真实漏洞，要么发现存在缺陷的规范需要精炼，从而对齐智能体理解与代码实际行为。

Result: 在真实世界测试中，Code-Augur有效利用安全规范检测出比现有最先进智能体更多的漏洞。此外，在关键开源项目中发现了22个新漏洞。与Claude Mythos等精心调优的专用模型相比，Code-Augur基于Sonnet、DeepSeek等广泛可用的LLM即可提供有效的智能体漏洞检测。

Conclusion: 安全规范优先范式通过显式化智能体的隐性假设并运行时证伪，有效提升了智能体漏洞检测的透明度和可靠性。Code-Augur证明，该方法不仅能发现更多漏洞，还能在通用LLM上实现不亚于甚至超过专用模型的检测能力，为智能体驱动的安全分析提供了更可信的路径。

Abstract: The advent of agentic vulnerability detection is already becoming a watershed moment for software security. Audits conducted entirely by autonomous LLM agents are uncovering critical vulnerabilities in fundamental software underpinning digital society. Many of these vulnerabilities remained masked for years, surfacing only now with AI agents. Yet the reasoning behind these discoveries remains alarmingly opaque and unvalidated. What assumptions did the agent make about a function's inputs when it deemed that function to be secure? Failures in reasoning and incorrect assumptions can lead to missed vulnerabilities and reduce trust in agentic analysis.
  We propose a security-specification-first paradigm that (1) exposes the agent's tacit assumptions explicitly as security specifications and (2) continuously refines those specifications via runtime falsification. We realize our approach in Code-Augur, a novel harness for agentic vulnerability detection. Given a codebase, Code-Augur analyzes each component of the system for vulnerable code. When it deems a component to be secure, it commits the local invariants behind that judgment as in-source assertions. In parallel, Code-Augur leverages a guided fuzzer to attempt to falsify those assumptions. When the fuzzer triggers an assertion, this either reveals a genuine vulnerability or a flawed specification to refine. In both cases, this process grounds the agent's understanding, aligning its view of code intent with how the code actually behaves. On real-world subjects, Code-Augur effectively leverages security specifications to detect more vulnerabilities than other state-of-the-art agents. Additionally, Code-Augur found 22 new vulnerabilities in key open-source projects. Compared to curated specialized models like Claude Mythos, Code-Augur offers effective agentic vulnerability detection built on widely available LLMs like Sonnet and DeepSeek.

</details>


### [178] [TGCM: Topic-Guided Generative Disentanglement of Interleaved APT Technique Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18651)
*Guo-Wei Wong,Ming-Chuan Yang,Shou-De Lin,Wang-Chien Lee,Meng~Chang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TGCM框架，利用一致性模型和MITRE ATT&CK主题引导先验，解决企业环境中多APT攻击流交织序列的未知数量解混问题，在合成和真实数据集上优于15种基线方法，并展现出零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中多个APT攻击活动并发进行，产生的审计日志中不同攻击源的技术序列相互交织，现有方法（统计模式挖掘、溯源分析等）通常假设单攻击场景或依赖刚性启发式规则，在多攻击重叠、技术共享、变长执行等现实条件下效果有限。

Method: 提出主题引导一致性建模（TGCM）框架：1）利用一致性模型学习从交织多攻击观测到结构化单攻击序列的直接逆映射，单步推理完成解混；2）引入基于MITRE ATT&CK叙述的主题引导先验，在分解过程中提供高层战术约束以保证语义连贯的攻击链。

Result: 在合成数据集、混合数据集及DARPA TC-E3/TC-E5上评估，与15种基线（模式挖掘、深度学习、LLM方法）对比，在高度交织和技术共享场景下分离鲁棒性显著提升，并在未训练的DARPA TC-E5真实交织基准上实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: TGCM有效解决了未知数量交织序列解混问题，为多源APT攻击的审计日志分析提供了生成式解耦新范式，具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In enterprise environments, multiple Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) campaigns often unfold concurrently, producing audit logs in which attack techniques across actors (sources) are interleaved over time. This setting naturally gives rise to an Unknown-K Interleaved Sequence Demixing (UKISD) problem: recovering multiple latent campaigns from an interleaved technique sequence while jointly inferring their number and technique-level assignments. Existing approaches, ranging from statistical pattern mining to provenance-based analysis, typically assume single-campaign settings or rely on rigid heuristics, limiting their effectiveness under realistic conditions involving overlapping campaigns, shared techniques, and variable execution lengths.
  We present Topic-Guided Consistency Modeling (TGCM), a generative disentanglement framework to tackle the UKSID problem. TGCM leverages Consistency Models to learn a direct inverse mapping from interleaved multi-campaign observations to structured single-campaign sequences in a single inference step. To favor semantically coherent attack chains, TGCM incorporates a topic-guided prior derived from MITRE ATT\&CK narratives, providing high-level tactical constraints during decomposition. We evaluate TGCM on synthetic datasets, established mixed datasets, and incident traces from DARPA TC-E3 and TC-E5, comparing against 15 representative baselines spanning pattern mining, deep learning, and LLM-based methods. Results indicate improved separation robustness over baselines under heavy interleaving and technique sharing, and show that TGCM generalizes zero-shot to a naturally interleaved in-the-wild benchmark (DARPA TC-E5) without retraining.

</details>


### [179] [Understanding and Mitigating Prompt Leaking Attacks in Real-World LLM-Based Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18673)
*Yong Yang,Chong Fu,Tong Zhang,Rui Zeng,Qingming Li,Tianyu Du,Zonghui Wang,Shouling Ji,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性研究了真实世界LLM应用中的系统提示泄露问题。对1200个应用进行测量发现超过80%存在泄露风险，现有防御措施往往失效。通过注意力机制分析揭示了注意力漂移（attention drift）是根本原因，并提出AREA防御方法——通过可优化软提示重新锚定模型注意力，在保持强抗泄露能力的同时将可用性提升33%以上，优化开销降低近3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示（system prompt）编码了LLM应用的核心逻辑和开发者定义的约束，是重要的知识产权，但容易被提示泄露攻击（prompt leaking attacks）窃取。尽管已有工作在受控环境下展示了此类攻击，但它们在真实部署中的普遍性、成因和防御措施仍不清楚，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 1) 对六大主流商业平台上的1,200个应用进行大规模测量实验，使用现实对抗性查询评估系统提示泄露程度。2) 进行注意力层级的机制分析（mechanistic analysis），识别出注意力漂移现象——查询-键对齐偏差（query-key alignment bias）和softmax放大导致LLM逐步忽略防御约束。3) 基于该洞察提出AREA防御，通过可优化的软提示（optimizable soft prompt）重新锚定模型注意力。

Result: 超过80%的部署在现实对抗性查询下泄露系统提示，有时暴露敏感信息如第三方API密钥。现有防御常以牺牲可用性为代价仍无法有效防泄露。AREA在抗泄露能力上与最先进的防御方法相当，同时将平均可用性提高33%以上，优化开销减少近3倍。负责任披露后，两家受影响供应商将此类泄露列为中等严重性漏洞。

Conclusion: 系统提示泄露在现实LLM应用中普遍存在且危害严重，现有防御存在根本性缺陷。注意力漂移是导致防御失效的机制性原因。AREA方法基于该机制洞察实现了更好的防御效果。该工作揭示了该安全问题的严重性并提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based applications rely on system prompts to encode core logic and developer-defined constraints, making these prompts important intellectual property. However, system prompts are vulnerable to prompt leaking attacks. Although prior work has shown such attacks in controlled settings, their prevalence, causes, and defenses in real-world deployments remain unclear.
  This paper presents a systematic study of prompt leaking in real-world LLM-based applications. We measure 1,200 applications across six major commercial platforms and find that over 80% of deployments leak system prompts under realistic adversarial queries, sometimes exposing sensitive information such as third-party API keys. We also show that existing defenses often fail to prevent leakage without degrading usability. To explain these failures, we conduct an attention-level mechanistic analysis and identify attention drift, where query-key alignment bias and softmax amplification cause LLMs to progressively ignore defensive constraints. Guided by this insight, we propose AREA, a practical defense that re-anchors the model's attention using an optimizable soft prompt. Experiments and real-world case studies show that AREA matches the leakage resistance of state-of-the-art defenses while improving average usability by over 33% and reducing optimization overhead by nearly 3x. Our responsible disclosure led two affected vendors to classify these leaks as medium-severity vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [180] [Image Prompt Reconstruction Attacks on Distributed MLLM Inference Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18710)
*Xinjian Luo,Hongyan Chang,Jianxin Wei,Yuncheng Wu,Xiaofeng Gao,Meikang Qiu,Ting Yu,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了分布式多模态大模型推理框架中，中间层嵌入导致输入图像隐私泄露的问题，提出两种被动黑盒图像重构攻击方法（MPAA和IEDA），在多个主流MLLM上验证了攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM推理框架中，中间嵌入已被证明会泄露文本提示的隐私。随着多模态大模型的发展，图像提示同样包含丰富的视觉和语义信息，其中间嵌入高度敏感。然而，分布式MLLM推理中的图像提示泄露问题尚未被探索。

Method: 首先分析图像像素到中间表示的信息流，设计图像嵌入提取算法作为重构攻击的前置条件（实验达到100%提取准确率）；在此基础上提出两种被动黑盒攻击：MPAA（通过逐块信息提取与组装实现细粒度像素级重建）和IEDA（通过嵌入引导的扩散生成实现粗粒度语义重建）。

Result: 在Gemma 3、Phi 4 Multimodal、Qwen 2.5 VL和Llama 4 Scout四个代表性MLLM家族上进行评估，展示了多种设置下一致优越的重构性能，并分析了MoE架构、图像预处理、模型规模和文本-图像依赖性对攻击性能的影响。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对MLLM的图像重构攻击研究，揭示了分布式MLLM推理中图像隐私面临的新威胁。

Abstract: Distributed large language model (LLM) inference frameworks connect isolated consumer-grade devices for large-scale model inference, substantially reducing hardware constraints. However, recent studies show that intermediate embeddings transmitted among participants can leak private prompts. As LLMs evolve into multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), this risk extends beyond text: image prompts contain rich visual and semantic information, making their intermediate embeddings highly privacy-sensitive. Yet, image-prompt leakage in distributed MLLM inference remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we investigate privacy risks to input images caused by intermediate embeddings in distributed MLLM frameworks. We first analyze the information flow from image pixels to intermediate representations. Since image and text embeddings are often intertwined across MLLM layers, we design an image embedding extraction algorithm as a prerequisite for reconstruction attacks, achieving 100% extraction accuracy across almost all MLLM layers in our experiments. Building on this, we develop two passive black-box image reconstruction attacks, MPAA and IEDA, reflecting realistic threats from normal participants with limited knowledge and capability. MPAA performs fine-grained pixel-level reconstruction via patch-wise information extraction and assembly, while IEDA performs coarse-grained semantic reconstruction through embedding-guided diffusion generation.
  We evaluate our attacks on four representative MLLM families: Gemma 3, Phi 4 Multimodal, Qwen 2.5 VL, and Llama 4 Scout. Results show consistently superior reconstruction performance in various settings. We further analyze the effects of MoE architecture, image preprocessing, model size, and text-image dependency on attack performance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of image reconstruction attacks on MLLMs.

</details>


### [181] [A Predictive Neural Network Architecture for Early Detection of Low-Rate Cyberattacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18771)
*Mert Nakıp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IDQS，一种基于QoS预测的轻量级框架，通过RTP-QoS神经网络预测流量质量并结合PDM决策模型识别异常，在IoT环境下实现低延迟（0.28秒）和高检测率（79%-91%）的LDoS攻击检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDoS攻击具有隐蔽性和持续性，传统入侵检测系统难以有效检测，特别是在资源受限的IoT网络中，亟需一种轻量级、主动式的早期检测方法。

Method: 提出IDQS框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) RTP-QoS，一种递归趋势预测神经网络，用于学习和预测未来QoS；(ii) PDM，成对决策模型，通过比较预测QoS与实际QoS的差异来识别潜在攻击。

Result: 在SDN-SlowRate-DDoS和CIC-IDS2017数据集上，IDQS分别达到79%和91%以上的检测准确率，具有高召回率和低假阴性率，端到端推理时间仅0.28秒。

Conclusion: IDQS在资源受限的IoT环境中能够实现高效、实时的LDoS攻击检测，验证了其在实际部署中的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Low-Rate Denial of Service (LDoS) attacks pose a significant challenge to IoT networks due to their subtle and prolonged nature, often evading traditional intrusion detection systems. This paper presents IDQS (Intrusion Detection via QoS Prediction), a lightweight and proactive framework for early LDoS attack detection. IDQS integrates two new key components: (i) RTP-QoS, a Recurrent Trend Predictive Neural Network that learns and forecasts future Quality of Service (QoS) based on historical traffic patterns, and (ii) PDM, a Pairwise Decision Model that evaluates discrepancies between predicted and actual QoS to identify potential attacks. Evaluated on the public SDN-SlowRate-DDoS and CIC-IDS2017 datasets, IDQS respectively achieves over 79% and 91% detection accuracy across most attack scenarios with high recall and low false negatives, while maintaining an end-to-end inference time of just 0.28 seconds. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of IDQS for real-time deployment in resource-constrained IoT environments.

</details>


### [182] [TRAP: Benchmark for Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18996)
*Moon Ye-Bin,Nam Hyeon-Woo,Baek Seong-Eun,Yejin Yeo,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAP评估框架，发现所有大模型在文档密集型任务中都会泄露隐私信息，指令遵循能力与泄露率正相关；理论上证明软约束防御无法同时实现高任务准确率和零泄露，提出结构化的私密字段隔离方法（用哈希键替换私密字段）可在保持任务准确率的同时有效防止泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代理系统处理文档密集型工作流（如订票需要护照号）时，代理必须使用私密信息完成任务但不得在响应中暴露这些信息，因为无法验证键盘前的人是谁。这两个义务存在根本性冲突：一个足够有能力使用私密信息完成任务的模型，同样有能力被诱导泄露这些信息。需要评估任务准确性与隐私泄露之间的权衡。

Method: 提出TRAP（Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction）评估框架，每个场景包含含私密信息的文档、要求代理使用私密字段调用正确工具的任务查询、以及试图在自然语言中诱导相同信息的攻击查询。评估了22个前沿闭源和开源模型。理论上证明对任何softmax模型，软约束防御无法同时实现高任务成功率和零泄露概率。提出结构化的私密字段隔离方法，在模型处理前用哈希键替换私密字段。

Result: 所有模型家族都表现出非平凡的泄露；指令遵循能力与泄露率相关；现有基于提示的防御降低了泄露但以显著牺牲任务准确率为代价；提示优化无法跳出这一权衡。结构化的私密字段隔离方法在很大程度上防止了泄露，同时保持了任务准确率。

Conclusion: 对于任何softmax模型，软约束防御无法同时实现高任务成功率和零泄露概率；结构化的私密字段隔离是解决这一矛盾的可行方案。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly deployed in document-intensive workflows where sensitive private information is not an edge case but a routine input, e.g., an agent booking a flight needs passport numbers. In such settings, the agent must use private information to complete tasks accurately while never exposing it in its responses, because it cannot verify who is actually at the keyboard. These two obligations are in fundamental tension. A model capable enough to use private information for task completion can, by the same capability, be induced to reveal it. To evaluate the trade-off of task accuracy and privacy leakage, we introduce Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction (TRAP). Each scenario includes a document containing private information, a task query that requires the agent to invoke the correct tool using private fields, and an attack query that attempts to elicit the same information in natural language. Evaluating 22 models spanning frontier proprietary and open-source models at multiple scales, we find that all model families exhibit non-trivial leakage, and that instruction-following ability correlates with leakage rate. Existing prompt-based defenses reduce leakage but at significant cost to task accuracy. Prompt optimization fails to escape this trade-off. We demonstrate that this failure is not incidental. For any softmax-based model, no soft-constraint defense, e.g., prompt-based defenses, can jointly achieve high task success with zero leakage probability. Motivated by this impossibility result, we propose structural private field isolation, which replaces private fields with hash keys before they reach the model. This approach largely prevents leakage while keeping task accuracy.

</details>


### [183] [Lifecycle-Aware Dynamic Analysis for Secure ML Model Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19023)
*Gabriele Digregorio,Marco Di Gennaro,Francesco Pastore,Stefano Zanero,Stefano Longari,Michele Carminati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Moat，一种动态生命周期感知的ML模型执行安全方法，通过监控模型执行各阶段与主机系统的结构化交互来检测恶意行为，在77,974个真实模型、31个CVE PoC和334个模型上实现零误报检测所有攻击类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型扫描方案依赖静态、格式特定的规则或已知攻击签名，难以跨框架泛化且无法检测新型攻击路径，亟需一种能泛化检测模型执行中恶意行为的方法。

Method: 提出Moat，一种动态生命周期感知方法，基于ML模型执行具有明确生命周期阶段且各阶段与主机系统交互高度结构化、可预测的观察，通过监控模型执行过程中的系统交互行为来检测攻击；实现为Re-Moat参考系统。

Result: 在77,974个Hugging Face Hub真实模型、31个CVE概念验证和334个SOTA数据集模型上评估，检测所有攻击类别，误报率接近零，优于现有模型扫描方案。

Conclusion: 动态生命周期感知分析是有效的ML模型执行安全方案，验证了基于执行行为而非静态特征检测恶意模型的可行性。

Abstract: The growing reliance on pre-trained Machine Learning (ML) models has introduced new attack surfaces. Recent vulnerabilities demonstrate that malicious behavior can be embedded within model artifacts, often bypassing existing defenses. Current model-scanning solutions primarily rely on static, format-specific rules or known attack signatures, which limit their ability to generalize across frameworks and to detect novel exploitation paths. In contrast, we propose a solution that focuses on the effects an attack has on the host system executing the model and builds on foundational intuitions about ML model execution. In particular, we observe that ML models operate within well-defined lifecycle phases and that, within each phase, interactions with the host system are highly structured and predictable. We translate these intuitions into Moat, a dynamic lifecycle-aware approach for securing ML model execution, and instantiate this design in Re-Moat, our reference implementation. We evaluate Re-Moat across multiple ML frameworks using 77,974 real-world model artifacts from the Hugging Face Hub, 31 Proofs-of-Concept (PoCs) from CVEs, and 334 models from a state-of-the-art dataset, and compare it against state-of-the-art model-scanning solutions. Our results show that our approach detects all evaluated attack classes while maintaining a close-to-zero false-positive rate, validating our intuitions and motivating dynamic analysis for securing ML model execution.

</details>


### [184] [PYPILINE: Malicious PyPI Package Detection via Suspicious API Knowledge and Agent Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19063)
*Siyuan Pang,Zhengwei Jiang,Yepeng Yao,Zijing Fan,Haozhe Li,Baoxu Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PYPILINE方法，结合可疑API知识库与Agent工作流，通过静态分析提取API知识库并利用语义分析检测恶意PyPI包，在精度96.7%、召回99.6%、F1 98.1%上显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意PyPI包检测方法主要依赖规则或传统机器学习，存在可解释性差且难以适应新型攻击的问题，亟需更有效的检测方案。

Method: 先对已知恶意包进行静态分析，提取抽象语法树并生成API调用图，自动构建结构化可疑API知识库；检测阶段通过Agent工作流结合知识库对未知包进行深度语义分析，输出结构化、可解释的恶意性评估报告。

Result: 精度96.7%、召回99.6%、F1 98.1%，精度超出基线工具5.7-24.2个百分点；同时揭示了主流攻击策略及最常被滥用的API。

Conclusion: PYPILINE结合可疑API知识库与Agent工作流，提供了一种实用、高效且便捷的恶意包检测解决方案，有效增强了开源生态系统安全性。

Abstract: The detection of malicious PyPI packages is crucial for maintaining the security of the open source software supply chain. Existing methods, which primarily rely on rules or traditional machine learning, suffer from poor interpretability and difficulty in adapting to novel attacks. To address this, we propose PYPILINE, a novel detection method that combines a suspicious API knowledge base with an Agent workflow. PYPILINE first conducts static analysis on known malicious packages, extracting abstract syntax trees and generating API call graphs, from which it automatically extracts and constructs a structured suspicious API knowledge base. During the detection phase, this knowledge base is used to enhance reasoning capabilities. Through an Agent workflow, PYPILINE performs in depth semantic analysis of unknown packages and outputs a structured, interpretable maliciousness assessment report. The experimental results show that PYPILINE significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art tools in precision of 96.7\%, recall of 99.6\%, and F1-score of 98.1\%, with its precision surpassing baseline tools by 5.7 to 24.2 percentage points. Additionally, we conducted an empirical study on malicious packages, systematically revealing prevalent attack strategies, as well as the most commonly abused APIs. Equipped with tool-calling AI agent workflows for automated vector database retrieval of suspicious API knowledge and mail server delivery of analysis reports, PYPILINE delivers a practical, efficient, and convenient malicious package detection solution to strengthen open-source ecosystem security.

</details>


### [185] [Compute-Budgeted Exploitability Evidence Graphs for Prospective Vulnerability Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19076)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究漏洞利用性预测的前瞻性评估问题，提出基于时间证据图的预算约束漏洞分类方法，通过可审计的证据证书实现泄漏安全的评估协议。实验表明，预算约束的证据选择能提升前瞻性召回率，且随机分割会严重高估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御者无法同时修补所有新披露漏洞，因此漏洞利用性预测必须以前瞻性（prospective）而非回顾性（retrospective）方式进行评估。现有方法常使用未来信息造成数据泄漏，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 将公告、漏洞利用存档、修复提交、黑客社区讨论等表示为时间证据图；采用预算约束选择器限制每个CVE的证据文档数量；每个评分附带可审计证书（含支持信号、时间戳、来源层和泄漏标志）。使用12012个前瞻性CVE进行实验。

Result: 预算约束的证据选择将泄漏安全的前瞻性召回率@50从0.010（仅严重性基线）提升至0.026；每个CVE两份证据文档即可捕获大部分价值；交叉编码器重排序器将前瞻性召回率降至0.016，表明语义相关性与利用性证据不同；随机分割使表观前瞻性召回率膨胀8.5倍，EPSS-high召回率膨胀5.0倍。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出了泄漏安全的评估协议和可重现的证据证书，用于可争议的漏洞优先级排序声明。

Abstract: Defenders cannot patch every newly disclosed vulnerability at once, so exploitability prediction must be evaluated prospectively rather than retrospectively. We study compute-budgeted vulnerability triage in which each CVE is scored only from public evidence visible by a fixed decision time. Advisories, exploit archives, fix commits, and hacker-community discourse are represented as a temporal evidence graph; a budgeted selector admits only a few evidence documents per CVE, and every score is paired with an auditable certificate listing the supporting signals, timestamps, source layers, and leakage flags. On 12012 prospective CVEs from public sources, budgeted evidence selection raises leakage-safe prospective recall@50 from 0.010 for a severity-only baseline to 0.026, while two evidence documents per CVE capture most of the value. A strong cross-encoder reranker lowers prospective recall to 0.016, showing that semantic relevance to a CVE is not the same as evidence of exploitation. Most importantly, a naive random split with unfiltered evidence inflates apparent prospective recall by 8.5x and EPSS-high recall by 5.0x. The main contribution is a leakage-safe evaluation protocol and reproducible evidence certificates for contestable vulnerability-prioritization claims.

</details>


### [186] [Quantifying Compromise Risk in Exceptional Access Architectures Under Sparse and Indirect Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19106)
*Alan Woodward*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个结构化不确定性框架，用于评估合法例外访问（EA）架构中的系统性泄露风险。通过对传输层EA（T-EA）和平台层EA（OTT-EA）进行四层分析（历史类比、蒙特卡洛场景、信道独立性分解及贝叶斯结构风险模型），发现：两类EA架构的泄露风险均严格高于无EA基线；T-EA风险由中心趋势主导，OTT-EA风险由尾部事件主导；T-EA的年泄露概率范围为1.4%-12.9%。密钥材料外泄不可逆，且随着时间累积，泄露概率显著高于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于合法例外访问系统风险的 debate 长期停留在定性层面，缺乏公开的EA特定泄露事件数据集，且过往研究将结构不同的EA设计（传输层EA与平台层EA）视为等效，未能区分其密钥与密文关系的根本差异。亟需一个结构化、可量化的风险评估框架。

Method: 构建四层分析框架：①历史类比（经验证据）；②蒙特卡洛场景层（模拟不确定性）；③信道独立性分解（分析密钥与密文关联关系）；④基于并行子图攻击图的贝叶斯结构风险模型。应用于T-EA和OTT-EA两类架构。

Result: EA架构的泄露风险严格高于无EA基线（该排序对假设不敏感）。T-EA风险由中心趋势主导，OTT-EA在关联攻击下由尾部风险主导。T-EA年泄露概率在结构化判断区间内为1.4%-12.9%。多十年累积泄露概率显著高于零。密钥材料外泄不可逆。

Conclusion: 框架可量化泄露概率而非预期危害，结论表明无论何种EA架构均引入额外系统风险，且T-EA与OTT-EA的风险分布形态本质不同，这对政策设计有重要含义。后果建模与效益估算不在本文范围内。

Abstract: Lawful exceptional access (EA) systems hold the cryptographic keys that decrypt protected communications for authorised parties. The debate over their risks has been long and qualitative, complicated by two problems: no public dataset of EA-specific compromise events exists, so assessment must use sparse, indirect evidence; and prior work has treated structurally different designs as equivalent, though transmission-layer EA in carrier infrastructure (T-EA) and over-the-top EA at the platform layer (OTT-EA) differ in how cryptographic keys relate to ciphertext data. This paper builds a structured uncertainty framework for evaluating systemic compromise risk in EA architectures. It does not produce predictive forecasts, which the evidence cannot support; it separates findings robust to assumptions from those that depend on calibration. Four analytical layers are applied to T-EA and OTT-EA: three empirical pillars (historical analogues, a Monte Carlo scenario layer, a channel-independence decomposition) plus a Bayesian Structural Risk Model on a parallel-subgraph attack graph. The central findings are structural. First, EA-equipped architectures of either class carry strictly higher modelled risk than their no-EA counterfactual, an ordering independent of calibration. Second, the classes differ in distribution shape: T-EA risk is dominated by central tendency, OTT-EA by the tail under correlated campaigns. Third, calibration-conditional annual probability ranges span 1.4% to 12.9% for T-EA across the structured-judgement targeting-premium interval.
