Over multi-decade horizons, cumulative compromise is well above zero; key-material exfiltration is irreversible, weighing heavily on OTT-EA's larger user populations. The framework quantifies compromise probability, not expected harm; consequence modelling and benefit estimation are outside its scope.

</details>


### [187] [Giskard : Byzantine Robust and Confidential Aggregation for Large-Scale Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19129)
*Ousmane Touat,César Sabater,Mohamed Maouche,Sonia Ben Mokhtar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Giskard 提出了一种面向去中心化学习的协议，通过将 n 个参与方组织成大小为 O(log n) 的委员会树，并利用 BGW 风格的安全多方计算（MPC）在委员会内执行坐标自适应中位数的分布式二分搜索，实现了机密性与拜占庭鲁棒性的兼顾，显著降低了每方的通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中同时处理机密性和拜占庭行为是困难的：机密性要求隐藏交换的模型参数/梯度（如使用密码学技术），而拜占庭鲁棒性需要检查这些参数/梯度，二者存在根本冲突。现有方案要么扩展性差（需要全对全通信），要么将计算委托给少数节点导致通信负载随网络规模线性增长。

Method: 将 n 个参与方组织成树形结构的委员会，每个委员会大小为 O(log n)；在委员会内通过 BGW 风格的 MPC 执行坐标自适应中位数的分布式二分搜索，实现鲁棒聚合。

Result: 理论层面证明了安全性和机密性属性；实验层面在多达 100 万参与者的规模下进行了评估。与最接近的竞争对手相比，Giskard 渐近地降低了每方的通信复杂度，并在最多 n/4 个拜占庭方的情况下保持了相当的模型效用。

Conclusion: Giskard 是首个在去中心化学习中同时实现机密性和拜占庭鲁棒性且具备良好扩展性的协议，通过委员会树结构和 MPC 的巧妙结合解决了二者之间的根本矛盾。

Abstract: Dealing simultaneously with confidentiality and Byzantine behaviors in decentralized learning is a challenging problem. Indeed, in decentralized learning, clients train a machine learning model while keeping their data locally and share their model parameters or gradients with a set of neighbors. While enforcing confidentiality calls for hiding the exchanged model parameters/gradients (e.g., by using cryptographic techniques), dealing with Byzantine contributions often requires inspecting the latter. Hence, most research works address these objectives separately. A recent line of work proposes to employ secure multi-party computation (MPC) to implement robust aggregators against model poisoning, thereby enforcing both confidentiality and Byzantine resilience. However, these solutions scale badly: they either require all-to-all communication between participants or delegate the entire computation to a small subset, whose computational and communication load grows proportionally with the size of the network.
  In this paper, we present Giskard, a protocol for confidential and Byzantine-robust decentralized aggregation. Giskard organizes $n$ parties into a tree of committees of size $O(\log n)$ and evaluates a coordinate-wise approximate median via a committee-adapted distributed binary search over the value domain, using BGW-style MPC within each committee. We assess Giskard both theoretically by proving its security and confidentiality properties and experimentally through extensive experiments involving up to one million participants. Compared to its closest competitors, Giskard reduces per-party communication complexity asymptotically while exhibiting comparable model utility under up to $n/4$ Byzantine parties.

</details>


### [188] [OpenAnt: LLM-Powered Vulnerability Discovery Through Code Decomposition, Adversarial Verification, and Dynamic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19149)
*Nahum Korda,Gadi Evron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OpenAnt 是一个开源漏洞发现系统，通过将静态程序分析与 LLM 推理相结合的多阶段流水线，实现可扩展的自动化安全分析。该方法将代码库分解为可达性过滤的分析单元（减少97%分析面），通过对抗性验证评估可利用性，并自动生成动态验证环境。在 OpenSSL、WordPress 和 Flowise 等项目中发现了未知漏洞，同时降低了误报率和分析成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析误报率高，动态分析（如模糊测试）基础设施需求大且覆盖漏洞类型窄；LLM 虽具备语义推理能力，但在仓库级安全分析中面临上下文管理、成本和验证等挑战。现有方法缺乏可扩展、低成本且高精度的自动化漏洞发现方案。

Method: OpenAnt 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 代码库分解为自包含分析单元，通过从外部入口点的可达性过滤，将分析面减少高达97%；(2) 候选漏洞通过受限攻击者模拟进行对抗性验证，评估在现实攻击能力下的可利用性；(3) 发现结果通过动态验证确认，自动生成漏洞利用环境并在沙箱容器中执行后丢弃。

Result: 在 OpenSSL、WordPress 和 Flowise 等广泛使用的开源项目中发现了先前未知的漏洞，同时保持了可控的分析成本并显著降低了误报率。

Conclusion: 将语义推理与漏洞利用验证相结合的闭环漏洞发现流水线，为实现可扩展的自动化安全分析提供了可行路径。OpenAnt 已在 Apache 2.0 许可下开源发布。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability discovery in large codebases remains challenging: traditional static analysis produces high false-positive rates, while dynamic approaches such as fuzzing require substantial infrastructure and often target narrow classes of bugs. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable semantic reasoning about program behavior, but applying LLMs to repository-scale security analysis introduces challenges related to context management, cost, and verification.
  We present OpenAnt, an open-source vulnerability discovery system that integrates static program analysis with LLM-based reasoning in a multi-stage pipeline. OpenAnt introduces three key techniques. First, codebases are decomposed into self-contained analysis units filtered by reachability from external entry points, reducing the analysis surface by up to 97% while preserving attack-relevant code. Second, candidate vulnerabilities undergo adversarial verification through constrained attacker simulation, where the model evaluates exploitability under realistic attacker capabilities. Third, findings are validated through dynamic verification, in which exploit environments are generated automatically, executed in sandboxed containers, and discarded after use.
  Evaluation on widely used open-source projects including OpenSSL, WordPress, and Flowise shows that this architecture can identify previously unknown vulnerabilities while maintaining manageable analysis cost and substantially reducing false positives. Our results suggest that closed-loop vulnerability discovery pipelines, combining semantic reasoning with exploit validation, provide a practical path toward scalable automated security analysis.
  OpenAnt is released as open source under the Apache 2.0 license at https://github.com/knostic/OpenAnt.

</details>


### [189] [PhantomSkill: Malicious Code Injection in Agent Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19191)
*Yu-Ting Lin,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PhantomSkill攻击框架，利用VulMask技术将恶意行为隐藏在技能辅助资源中而非文本描述，通过重写恶意脚本为漏洞形式，仅在攻击者控制条件下激活，绕过现有检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体通过第三方包获取领域技能时，技能引入的新型供应链攻击面尚未被充分研究，现有防御主要关注文本描述的恶意意图，忽略了隐藏在辅助资源中的攻击。

Method: 提出PhantomSkill框架，核心是VulMask技术，将显式恶意脚本重写为漏洞形式（vulnerability-shaped implementations），恶意行为仅在攻击者控制的触发条件下激活，将可见信号从明确恶意意图转变为看似普通的脆弱代码。

Result: 在多种宿主技能、攻击目标、编码智能体、生成模型和自动审查器上，VulMask在保持良性实用性的同时，相比显式恶意脚本显著降低了警告和恶意软件级别检测率。

Conclusion: 技能生态系统需要资源级审查、执行时隔离以及将智能体技能中可利用漏洞视为潜在恶意载荷的安全策略。

Abstract: Agent skills allow LLM-based coding agents to acquire domain-specific capabilities from third-party packages, but they also introduce a new supply-chain attack surface. We present PhantomSkill, an attack framework that hides malicious behavior in a skill's auxiliary resources rather than in its textual description. Its core technique, VulMask, rewrites overt malicious scripts into vulnerability-shaped implementations whose malicious behavior is activated only under attacker-controlled trigger conditions. This design shifts the visible signal from explicit malicious intent to ordinary-looking insecure code. Across representative host skills, attack goals, coding agents, generation models, and automated reviewers, VulMask preserves benign utility while reducing warning and malware-level detection compared with overt malicious scripts. Our results show that skill ecosystems require resource-level vetting, execution-time containment, and security policies that treat exploitable vulnerabilities in agent skills as potential malicious payloads.

</details>


### [190] [CodeSentinel: A Three-Layer Defense Against Indirect Prompt Injection in Code Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19235)
*Po-Han Cheng,Chia-Mu Yu,Ying-Dar Lin,Yu-Sung Wu,Wei-Bin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeSentinel，一种三层推理时净化器，用于检测和移除代码大语言模型中由间接提示注入引起的恶意内容。它通过Tree-sitter提取高风险节点，结合语法引导预过滤、CST引导动态Min-K%评分和节点扰动分析，在六种攻击家族上达到0.80的节点级F1，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码大语言模型越来越多地从仓库、文档、问题线程和编码代理环境中检索外部代码上下文，形成了间接提示注入攻击面，攻击者可以将恶意指令隐藏在注释、字符串、标识符或诱饵代码中，威胁模型安全。

Method: CodeSentinel采用三层推理时净化架构：1) 使用Tree-sitter提取高风险模型面向的CST节点；2) 组合语法引导预过滤、CST引导动态Min-K%评分和节点扰动分析；3) 检测到的节点在到达下游Code LLM前被移除或中和。

Result: 在六种近期攻击家族上，CodeSentinel达到0.80的平均节点级F1分数，优于CodeGarrison、DePA和KillBadCode等基线方法。

Conclusion: CodeSentinel通过多层推理时净化策略有效防御代码LLM的间接提示注入攻击，在检测性能上显著超越现有防御方法。

Abstract: Code large language models increasingly retrieve external code context from repositories, documentation, issue threads, and coding-agent environments, creating an indirect prompt-injection surface where attackers hide instructions in comments, strings, identifiers, or decoy code. We propose CodeSentinel, a three-layer inference-time sanitizer. It uses Tree-sitter to extract high-risk model-facing CST nodes, then combines syntax-guided pre-filtering, CST-guided Dynamic Min-K\% scoring, and node perturbation analysis to detect adversarial and natural-looking semantic triggers. Detected nodes are removed or neutralized before reaching the downstream Code LLM. Across six recent attack families, \CodeSentinel achieves 0.80 average node-level F1, outperforming CodeGarrison, DePA, and KillBadCode.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Recover, Discover, Plan: Learning Skills and Concepts from Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18328)
*Bowen Li,Mayank Mishra,Y. Isabel Liu,Stone Tao,Nishanth Kumar,Alexander G. Gray,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Sebastian Scherer,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReSYNC是一种通过失败恢复经验逐步发现和优化关系谓词状态抽象的方法，结合强化学习技能学习与抽象概念学习，使机器人能从局部恢复行为泛化为全局故障避免，在四个模拟域和真实机器人任务中显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法仅能学习反应式恢复行为，但为每种故障模式单独训练策略效率极低；机器人需要不仅能从故障中恢复，更能获取抽象知识以主动避免未来故障。

Method: 采用增量式双学习过程：(1) 技能学习阶段——使用RL学习从训练任务中的故障恢复；(2) 概念学习阶段——发现新关系谓词并精炼抽象规划模型，以解释和泛化所学恢复行为；通过持续扩展和优化抽象库实现从局部恢复到全局故障避免的转化。

Result: 在四个模拟域中，ReSYNC解决长时域未见问题的能力超过强基线方法50%以上；并成功实现sim-to-real迁移，在真实世界完成非抓取操作技能并在未见场景中泛化。

Conclusion: ReSYNC是迈向机器人自主获取抽象知识以实现可扩展、故障感知规划的重要一步。

Abstract: Intelligent robots should not only recover from failures, but also acquire the abstract knowledge needed to avoid them in the future. While reinforcement learning (RL) can learn reactive recovery behaviors, training a separate policy for every distinct failure mode is highly inefficient. We introduce Recovery-Driven Synthesis of Relational Concepts (ReSYNC), the first approach that progressively discovers and refines state abstractions (relational predicates) from failure-recovery experience to support abstract planning. Unlike purely reactive methods, ReSYNC jointly learns skills and concepts through an incremental dual-learning process. In the skill-learning phase, the robot uses RL to learn to recover from failures seen in training tasks. In the concept-learning phase, the robot discovers new relational predicates and refines its abstract planning model to explain and generalize the learned recovery behaviors. This interaction enables ReSYNC to convert local recoveries seen during training into global failure avoidance at test time. Across four simulated domains, we show that ReSYNC's ability to continually expand and refine its abstraction library allows it to solve long-horizon, previously unseen problems, outperforming strong baselines by over 50%. Additionally, we demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of ReSYNC, where it performs real-world non-prehensile manipulation skills and generalizes to unseen scenarios through abstract planning. Overall, ReSYNC represents a significant step toward robots that autonomously acquire abstractions for scalable, failure-aware planning in the physical world.

</details>


### [192] [Guava: An Effective and Universal Harness for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18363)
*Haowen Liu,Xirui Li,Shaoxiong Yao,Peng Shi,Tianyi Zhou,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Guava框架，通过系统探索智能体工作流、动作空间和观察空间的设计空间，识别出迭代感知-推理-动作循环、语义动作抽象和多模态观测三个关键要素。基于少于2000条模拟轨迹的端到端训练流程，将具身操作能力蒸馏至4B开源模型，在仿真和真实环境中达到与前沿专有模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究如何有效设计框架（harness）以释放语言模型在具身操作中的能力，并验证良好设计的框架是否对多种推理模型（包括小模型）具有通用性，从而替代端到端视觉-语言-动作系统。

Method: 通过系统探索设计空间（智能体工作流、动作空间、观察空间），提出Guava框架；利用少于2000条模拟轨迹的端到端蒸馏训练流程，将能力转移至4B开源模型。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中达到与前沿专有模型相当的性能，展现出对未见物体、新指令和长时任务的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 精心设计的框架可作为可扩展、模型无关的具身操作接口，使紧凑的开源模型在极少训练数据下涌现出强大的具身能力。

Abstract: Language models trained on large-scale vision-language data have demonstrated strong potential for embodied agents. Harnessing models through embodied tools use offers a promising alternative to end-to-end vision-language-action systems by combining high-level reasoning with external modules for perception, planning, and control. However, it remains unclear what makes an effective harness for embodied manipulation, and to what extent such a harness can unlock embodied capabilities in a wide range of reasoning models. In this work, we present Guava, a harness framework for embodied tool use developed through systematic exploration of the design space of agent workflows, action spaces, and observation spaces. Our study identifies three key ingredients for effective embodied agents: iterative perception-reasoning-action loops, semantic action abstractions, and multimodal observations. To understand whether these design principles are universal even to small models, we develop an end-to-end training pipeline that distills embodied manipulation capabilities into a 4B open-source model using fewer than 2K trajectories collected entirely in simulation. Experimental results in both simulation and real-world environments show performance comparable to frontier proprietary models while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen objects, novel instructions, and long-horizon tasks. Results suggest that a well-designed harness can serve as a scalable, model-agnostic interface for embodied manipulation, enabling strong emergent embodied capabilities in compact open-source models with minimal training data.

</details>


### [193] [Hardware- and Vision-in-the-Loop Validation of Deep Monocular Pose Estimation for Autonomous Maritime UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19176)
*Maneesha Wickramasuriya,Beomyeol Yu,Jaden Shin,Mason Huslig,Taeyoung Lee,Murray Snyder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件在环的视觉闭环框架，通过渲染逼真的海洋环境图像、板载深度变压器单目位姿估计、延迟卡尔曼滤波融合视觉与IMU数据，实现了全自主室内飞行，为无人机海上舰船部署前的安全、硬件真实的中间测试阶段提供了验证平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在舰船上的自主作业需要可靠的视觉相对位姿估计，但海上实际验证成本高昂、受天气影响大且风险高，亟需一种安全、低成本且硬件真实的中间验证手段。

Method: 构建了视觉在环框架：(1) 渲染逼真的海上环境图像；(2) 板载深度变压器网络进行单目位姿估计；(3) 延迟卡尔曼滤波器融合低频视觉测量与高频IMU数据，处理感知延迟、异步更新和计算约束等嵌入式效应。

Result: 实现了自主起飞、轨迹跟踪和着陆实验，展示了稳定的闭环飞行性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为海上无人机自主性开发提供了一个安全、硬件真实的中间测试阶段，能够在实际舰船部署前有效验证算法与系统集成。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV operations on ships require reliable vision-based relative pose estimation, yet at-sea validation is costly, weather-dependent, and risky. This paper presents a hardware-validated vision-in-the-loop framework that enables fully autonomous indoor flight while emulating photorealistic maritime environments. Rendered maritime views are processed onboard by a deep transformer-based monocular pose estimator. Delayed vision measurements are fused with high-rate IMU data using a delayed Kalman filter to provide consistent state estimates for geometric control. The system captures critical embedded effects, including perception latency, asynchronous updates, and computational constraints, that are absent in pure simulation. Autonomous takeoff, trajectory tracking, and landing experiments demonstrate stable closed-loop flight. The results establish a safe and hardware-realistic intermediate stage for developing maritime UAV autonomy prior to shipboard deployment.

</details>


### [194] [PAIWorld: A 3D-Consistent World Foundation Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18375)
*Yuhang Huang,Xuan Lv,Junyan Xu,Zhiyuan Yu,Jiazhao Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Wancheng Feng,Shilong Zou,Hewen Xiao,Ziqiao Zhou,Kaiyun Huang,Zhiyu Peng,Juzhan Xu,Hang Zhao,Chenyang Zhu,Renjiao Yi,Yifei Huang,Douhui Wu,Yan Zhang,Kexu Cheng,Chunhe Song,Yunzhi Xue,Xiuhong Zhang,Leitao Guo,Yunji Chen,Bin Wu,Haibin Yu,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAIWorld通过几何感知交叉注意力、几何旋转位置编码和3D特征蒸馏，在扩散Transformer世界模型中实现了多视图3D一致性，在机器人操作基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单视图世界模型缺乏多视图3D一致性导致跨视角物体漂移、深度不一致和纹理错位，而多视图世界模型仅简单拼接视图token缺乏显式几何推理。

Method: 提出PAIWorld框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 几何感知交叉注意力块实现视图间显式通信；(2) 几何旋转位置编码将相机射线方向和外部位姿编码到注意力机制中；(3) 潜在3D-REPA从冻结的3D基础模型蒸馏3D感知特征保证3D一致性。

Result: 在机器人操作基准上达到SOTA多视图3D一致性，WorldArena排行榜第1名，AgiBot-Challenge2026排行榜第2名，并支持基于模型的规划、世界动作模型和多视图策略后训练等下游应用。

Conclusion: 同时解决显式视图间通信机制和3D几何先验缺失两个缺陷是必要且充分的，PAIWorld的成功验证了这一论点。

Abstract: World foundation models (WFMs) are powerful simulators, yet they predominantly operate in a single-view setting and lack the multi-view 3D consistency required for robotic manipulation. While robotic systems rely on multiple cameras (egocentric, eye-to-hand, and wrist-mounted) for policy learning, current multi-view world models simply concatenate view tokens without explicit geometric reasoning. This causes cross-view object drift, depth inconsistency, and texture misalignment. We trace these failures to two deficiencies: the absence of an explicit inter-view communication mechanism and the lack of a 3D geometric prior. We argue that resolving both simultaneously is necessary and sufficient. To address this, we present PAIWorld, a framework that augments diffusion-transformer world models via three core components: (1) Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention blocks that establish an explicit pathway across views, (2) Geometric Rotary Position Embedding that encodes camera ray directions and extrinsic poses into the attention mechanism, and (3) Latent 3D-REPA, which distills 3D-aware features from frozen 3D foundation models to ensure 3D consistency. Built upon a DiT-based world foundation model, PAIWorld achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D consistency on robotic manipulation benchmarks, ranking 1st on the WorldArena leaderboard and 2nd on the AgiBot-Challenge2026 leaderboard, while enabling downstream applications such as model-based planning, world action models, and multi-view policy post-training.

</details>


### [195] [Seeing Through Occlusion: Deterministic Arm Kinematic Correction for Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19240)
*Thomas M. Kwok,Nicholas Koenig,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于手臂恒定长度几何约束的深度校正方法（AKC），用于改善单RGB-D相机在自遮挡情况下的上肢运动捕捉深度估计，无需复杂概率建模，在静态/动态关节运动及机器人遥操作中均表现可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单RGB-D相机在存在自遮挡（尤其是上肢运动）时，深度估计会退化，影响遥操作性能。现有方法复杂或需要参数调优，缺乏一种简单、确定性的深度校正方案。

Method: 基于手臂长度恒定的几何约束，利用腕部位置和预定义臂长，通过勾股定理构建确定性公式重建被遮挡关节的深度，无需概率建模或参数调节。

Result: 以Vicon为参考，静态和动态关节运动的RMSE和皮尔逊相关系数表现可靠；在仿真和真实机器人环境中成功实现运动映射遥操作；在长时间严重自遮挡下仍保持解剖一致性，即便配合不可靠的时序滤波器也表现稳健。

Conclusion: AKC方法能有效增强单RGB-D相机在自遮挡场景下的深度估计鲁棒性和解剖一致性，适用于机器人遥操作和人机交互等实时应用。

Abstract: Markerless, single-RGB-D-camera motion capture provides a low-cost and non-invasive alternative to conventional marker-based systems for robot teleoperation; however, depth estimation often degrades in the presence of self-occlusion, particularly during upper-limb motion. This paper presents an Arm Kinematic Correction (AKC) method that improves depth estimation by enforcing geometric constraints based on constant arm lengths. The proposed approach reconstructs occluded joint depths by leveraging wrist positions and predefined arm lengths via a deterministic formulation based on the Pythagorean theorem, thereby avoiding the need for complex probabilistic modeling or parameter tuning. Experimental validation against a Vicon reference system demonstrates reliable performance for both static and dynamic joint motions, evaluated using root-mean-square error (RMSE) and Pearson correlation. Furthermore, motion-mapping teleoperation is successfully demonstrated in both simulated and physical robot environments. The results show that AKC enhances robustness and preserves anatomical consistency under long-duration, severe self-occlusion, even when paired with less reliable temporal filters, highlighting its practicality for real-time applications such as robot teleoperation and human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [196] [VEGA: Learning Navigation VLAs from In-the-Wild Egocentric Video with Geometric Trajectory Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18426)
*Gershom Seneviratne,Yohan Abeysinghe,Jianyu An,Vaibhav Shende,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VEGA是一种从无标签第一人称导航视频中训练导航视觉-语言-动作模型的方法，通过重建场景几何生成避障轨迹，在VEGA-Bench和真实环境中显著减少了碰撞并提升了避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的第一人称视频包含丰富的导航视觉观察，但缺乏机器人坐标系下基于明确导航目标的避障轨迹，无法直接用于策略学习。VEGA旨在弥合这一差距，使无标签视频可用于训练避障导航策略。

Method: VEGA从单目视频重建局部场景几何，采样导航目标（文本、图像或空间路径点），利用重建几何生成避障轨迹，然后用生成的轨迹分布训练流匹配VLA导航策略。几何信息仅在训练阶段使用，将避障规划蒸馏到纯视觉策略中。

Result: VEGA-Bench包含25万场景和约500万导航目标-几何配对。VEGA在VEGA-Bench上相比最强基线碰撞减少33.0%，障碍物间距提升17.9%；在真实测试中成功率提升至少150.0%，碰撞减少至少66.7%，障碍物间距提升至少60.0%。

Conclusion: 视频导出的几何监督为训练避障导航VLA提供了一种可扩展且有效的信号。

Abstract: We introduce VEGA, an approach for training navigation VisionLanguage-Action (VLA) models from unlabeled egocentric navigation videos. Internet-scale egocentric videos provide a scalable source of navigation-relevant visual observations, capturing cluttered scenes, close-range obstacles, and natural human motion through real-world spaces. However, these videos are not directly usable for policy learning because they do not provide obstacle-aware trajectories conditioned on explicit navigation goals in the robot's coordinate frame. VEGA addresses this gap by reconstructing local scene geometry from monocular video, sampling navigation goals (represented as text, image, or spatial waypoints) and generating obstacle-aware trajectories using the constructed geometry. The resulting trajectory distribution is then used to train a flow-matching VLA navigation policy. By using geometry exclusively during training, VEGA distills obstacle-aware planning directly into a vision-based policy. Furthermore, we introduce VEGA-Bench, a benchmark containing 250k scenes and approximately 5 million navigation goals paired with scene geometry, designed to evaluate goal progress, collision avoidance, and obstacle clearance of VLAs. Our evaluation shows that VEGA achieves competitive goal progress while reducing collisions by 33.0% and improving obstacle clearance by 17.9% over the strongest baseline on VEGABench, while improving success by at least 150.0%, reducing collisions by at least 66.7%, and improving obstacle clearance by at least 60.0% in real-world trials. Ultimately, we demonstrate that video-derived geometric supervision provides a scalable and effective signal for training obstacle-aware navigation VLAs. The code and benchmark will be released at the time of publication.

</details>


### [197] [A Mixed-Reality Testbed for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19267)
*H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Ehsan Sabouni,Emrullah Celik,Zean Wan,Damola Ajeyemi,Christos G. Cassandras,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合现实、硬件在环（HIL）的自动驾驶车辆测试平台，将物理移动机器人测试平台与高保真仿真环境无缝集成，支持多样化的安全关键驾驶场景验证、多模态传感器融合、车联网通信以及多智能体系统研究，并提出了结合感知、规划与基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的在线学习控制器的安全保证框架，实验验证了该平台在弥合仿真与现实部署之间差距的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶测试方法难以同时兼顾高保真仿真（安全关键场景构建）和真实硬件部署的可靠性，存在仿真与现实之间的鸿沟。需要一种能够融合物理机器人与虚拟仿真、支持多智能体协同且具备安全保证的混合测试平台。

Method: 提出混合现实HIL测试平台，集成物理移动机器人（配备多模态传感器）与高保真虚拟仿真环境；支持V2X无线通信；通过物理机器人与虚拟智能体组合实现大规模多智能体系统（含网联自动驾驶车辆CAVs）研究；提出结合感知、规划与基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的在线学习控制器的安全保证框架。

Result: 实验验证了所提测试平台的关键功能，包括安全关键场景构建、多模态传感器融合、车联网通信、多智能体协同以及CBF在线学习控制器的安全保证能力，证明了该平台能有效弥合仿真与现实硬件部署之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该混合现实HIL测试平台为自动驾驶车辆提供了一种兼具仿真灵活性与硬件真实性的验证手段，尤其适用于安全关键场景和多智能体系统研究。未来可进一步扩展其在大规模CAVs协同场景中的应用。

Abstract: We propose a mixed-reality, hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testbed for autonomous vehicles that seamlessly integrates a physical testbed of mobile robots with a high-fidelity simulation environment. The virtual simulation enables the creation of diverse, safety-critical driving scenarios to validate state-of-the-art perception, planning, and control algorithms, while augmenting simulations with physical robots equipped with multimodal sensors in photorealistic virtual environments further facilitating rigorous validation. Our testbed also features vehicular connectivity using wireless communication and can accommodate a large number of agents through the combination of physical robots and virtual simulated agents, supporting research on multi-agent systems including Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). Finally, we present a safety-guaranteed framework combining perception, planning and a novel online learning-based controller using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) for CAVs. Experiments using the proposed framework are used to validate and demonstrate the key functionalities and the overall utility of the testbed to bridge the gap between simulation and real-world hardware deployment.

</details>


### [198] [N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance Constraints to Solve Stochastic Orienteering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18514)
*Anas Saeed,Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出N(CO)²框架，将强化学习与概率约束相结合，用于解决随机定向问题(SOP)，无需手工设计启发式算法，在不确定环境下实现自适应路径优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化(NCO)主要研究确定性组合优化问题，对随机组合优化问题研究不足，传统方法依赖手工启发式算法，缺乏通用性和自适应性。

Method: 将概率约束(Chance Constraints)与强化学习框架相结合，构建N(CO)²模型，在不确定条件下优化路径选择，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 该方法在多种随机定向问题实例上具有良好的泛化能力，性能与最先进的混合整数线性规划(MILP)方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法减少了启发式设计中的人工劳动，能够在不确定环境中实现自适应和高效的决策。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) offers a promising alternative to traditional heuristic-based methods for solving complex graph optimization problems by proposing to learn heuristics through data. This class of problems frequently arises in automation, as it can be used to model a variety of applications. While NCO has been extensively studied for deterministic combinatorial optimization problems, there are only a few works that aim to solve stochastic combinatorial optimization problems. In this work, we present N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance cOnstraints to solve the Stochastic Orienteering Problem (SOP) without the use of hand-crafted heuristics. By integrating a reinforcement learning (RL) framework, the model optimizes path selection under uncertainty, effectively balancing exploration and exploitation. Empirical results demonstrate that our method generalizes well across diverse SOP instances, achieving competitive performance compared to the state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear program (MILP) for the task. The proposed approach reduces human effort in heuristic design while enabling adaptive and efficient decision-making in uncertain environments.

</details>


### [199] [Task Allocation and Motion Planning in Dynamic, Cluttered Environments via CBBA and Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18516)
*Matthew D. Osburn,Cameron K. Peterson,John L. Salmon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合凸集图(GCS)轨迹优化与共识捆绑算法(CBBA)分布式任务分配的方法，解决复杂动态环境下多智能体任务规划问题，在3D+时间配置空间中实现碰撞规避与高效任务分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中任务分配不仅取决于哪个智能体最适合某项任务，还取决于该任务在何时何地可达，现有方法难以同时处理轨迹优化与分布式任务分配的耦合问题。

Method: 将GCS用于动态环境下的时间扩展(3D+时间)配置空间轨迹优化，CBBA用于分布式任务分配，并将分配与规划相连接以实现碰撞规避并提供准确的任务完成时间估计。

Result: 在包含静态和动态任务的模拟复杂环境中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效结合轨迹优化与分布式任务分配，在动态环境中实现多智能体协同任务规划与碰撞规避。

Abstract: Multi-agent task planning in cluttered, dynamic environments requires assigning tasks to agents while simultaneously determining safe, time-efficient trajectories through the environment. When tasks are dynamic, such as rendezvous objectives, allocation decisions depend not only on which agent is best suited for a task, but also on when and where that task can be reached. This paper presents a solution to this problem, which combines Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) for trajectory optimization with the Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA) for distributed task allocation. In our approach, GCS finds optimal trajectories through dynamic environments using a time-extended (3D+time) configuration space. At the same time, CBBA coordinates task assignments across agents, enabling informed decision-making in a moving environment. We then connect allocation and planning to allow the agents to avoid collisions in the 3D+time configuration space and provide accurate time estimates for task completion. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in simulated cluttered environments with static and dynamic tasks.

</details>


### [200] [As You Wish: Mission Planning with Formal Verification using LLMs in Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18519)
*Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的精准农业任务规划系统，通过引入线性时序逻辑(LTL)和多反馈循环架构，并使用两个不同商业LLM分别负责规范生成与验证，以解决自然语言歧义性问题。实验展示了该自主验证管线的优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统高度专业化、操作门槛高，且基于自然语言的任务规划存在歧义性问题，需要一种能够自动验证规划结果是否满足用户规范的方法。

Method: 在原有LLM任务规划器基础上，引入多反馈循环架构，使用线性时序逻辑(LTL)进行规范验证；采用两个不同的商业LLM分别承担规范生成与验证子任务以减轻偏差，构建全自主管线。

Result: 通过大量实验验证了LTL公式生成效果、自主验证管线的优势与局限，展示了该方法在解决自然语言歧义性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 多反馈循环+LTL的双LLM架构能有效缓解自然语言歧义性对任务规划的影响，但LLM生成LTL公式的能力仍存在局限性。

Abstract: Though robotic systems are now being commercialized and deployed in various industries, many of these systems are highly specialized and often require an advanced skill set to operate and ensure they perform as instructed. To mitigate this problem, we recently introduced a mission planner leveraging LLMs to synthesize mission plans in precision agriculture based on mission descriptions provided in natural language. While the system demonstrates impressive performance, it also suffers from the inherent ambiguities of natural language. In this paper, we extend our system to address this issue by introducing multiple feedback loops in the planning architecture that leverage linear temporal logic (LTL) to ensure the mission planning system meets the specifications formulated by the user while still using natural language. To mitigate potential bias, this is achieved by using two different commercial LLMs in charge of the specification and verification subtasks. Through extensive experiments, we highlight the strengths and limitations of integrating mission verification into a fully autonomous pipeline, particularly regarding an LLM's ability to generate valuable LTL formulas, and show how our proposed implementation addresses and solves these challenges.

</details>


### [201] [DREAM-Chunk: Reactive Action Chunking with Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18589)
*Wenxi Chen,Kaidi Zhang,Chi Lin,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yu She,Yuejiang Liu,Raymond A. Yeh,Shaoshuai Mou,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DREAM-Chunk 是一种测试时扩展方法，通过在动作分块策略中引入轻量级潜空间世界模型，无需额外微调即可提升策略在随机动态下的鲁棒性。该方法在测试时采样多个候选动作块，预测其潜空间未来状态，并选择与真实观测最匹配的动作执行，从而应对随机性和部分可观测性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块策略在执行时是开环的，面对随机动力学、硬件执行误差和部分可观测性时表现脆弱，需要一种无需重新训练即可提升鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 在测试阶段，DREAM-Chunk 采样多个候选动作块，利用轻量级潜空间世界模型 rollout 预测每个候选块的未来潜状态，选择预测状态与真实观测最匹配的动作块执行，通过增加测试时计算量来覆盖多种可能的随机未来。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准测试上，DREAM-Chunk 在增大动作噪声时提升了鲁棒性，且受益于更大的候选样本量。在四个操作任务、两个机器人平台和两种 VLA 策略上的仿真与硬件实验中均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: DREAM-Chunk 通过测试时计算增强动作分块策略的鲁棒性，无需微调即可应对随机动态，是一种实用的测试时扩展方法。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common interface for vision-language-action (VLA) models, enabling low-frequency policy inference to drive high-frequency robot execution. However, once an action chunk is committed, its open-loop execution can be brittle under stochastic dynamics, hardware execution errors, and partial observability. We propose DREAM-Chunk, a test-time scaling method that augments chunking-based policies with a lightweight latent world model, without requiring additional policy fine-tuning. At test time, DREAM-Chunk samples multiple candidate action chunks, rolls out their predicted latent futures, and selects actions from the chunk whose predicted state best matches the observed rollout. In this way, DREAM-Chunk uses additional test-time computation to cover multiple plausible stochastic futures and improve reactivity during long-horizon chunk execution. On the Kinetix benchmark, DREAM-Chunk improves robustness under increasing action noise and benefits from larger candidate sample sizes, especially when demonstrations contain corrective behaviors. We further validate DREAM-Chunk on four manipulation tasks across two robot platforms and two VLA policies under various sources of stochasticity. Across simulation and hardware experiments, DREAM-Chunk improves the robustness of action-chunking policies in stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [202] [Benchmarking Action Spaces in Reinforcement Learning for Vision-based Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18594)
*Seyed Alireza Azimi,Homayoon Farrahi,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种动作空间表示（位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量、关节速度）在视觉抓取和推操作任务中的表现，发现关节速度动作空间在平滑性和最终任务性能上最优，并为RL实践者提供了动作空间选择的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界强化学习中，动作空间的选择对运动平滑性、安全性和任务性能有重要影响，但缺乏针对基于视觉的操作任务中不同动作空间表示的系统比较研究。

Method: 在仿真中训练策略，通过sim-to-real迁移部署到真实世界，在两种视觉操作任务（物体抓取和推动）上比较四种动作空间：位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量、关节速度。

Result: 关节速度动作空间在平滑性和最终任务性能上均最优；动作空间表示显著影响sim-to-real迁移性能。

Conclusion: 关节速度是视觉抓取和推动任务的最佳动作空间选择，同时为RL实践者提供了动作空间选择的实用指导。

Abstract: In real-world reinforcement learning (RL), the choice of action space can play a key role in shaping motion smoothness, safety, and overall task performance. In this study, we evaluate pose increment, pose velocity, joint position increment, and joint velocity across two vision-based manipulation tasks: object picking and pushing. We train policies in simulation and deploy them to the real world using sim-to-real transfer. We find that action-space representation indeed significantly affects sim-to-real performance. In particular, we find that the joint velocity action space is best for the vision-based picking and pushing tasks in terms of smoothness and final task performance. We also provide practical guidance for RL practitioners in choosing action spaces for both simulation and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [203] [Admittance-Based Surface Alignment for Human-in-the-Loop Robotic Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18601)
*Antara Banerjee,Colin Acton,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于导纳控制的实时闭环机器人定向控制框架，用于精密视觉检测，将操作员输入与感知驱动的表面对齐统一为虚拟球体在粘性介质中的运动，在6-DOF机械臂上实现了0.4°的平均定向误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密视觉检测中，机器人需要精确对齐末端执行器与局部表面几何，但工业场景中常有人类操作员通过遥操作或共享自治进行实时调整，纯离线运动规划无法应对，因此需要能够处理人类和感知不确定性的反应性、柔顺控制架构。

Method: 提出一种基于导纳控制的实时闭环定向控制管线，将末端执行器建模为在粘性介质中运动的虚拟球体，形成的质量-阻尼系统从定向误差和操作员指令中生成同步、柔顺的运动，统一了操作员输入与感知驱动的表面对齐。

Result: 在6-DOF机械臂上验证，展示了稳定的法向跟踪，最终平均定向误差为0.4°。

Conclusion: 所提出的导纳控制框架能有效结合人类操作与感知信息，实现精密视觉检测中精确、稳定的定向控制。

Abstract: Precision visual inspection underpins quality assurance across aerospace, semiconductor, and medical manufacturing, where undetected surface anomalies on high-value parts translate directly into scrap, rework, and field failures. Robotic visual inspection requires precise alignment between the end-effector and local surface geometry in the presence of perception noise and surface irregularities. In industrial settings, a human operator is often kept in the loop via teleoperation or shared autonomy, introducing real-time adjustments that render purely offline motion planning inadequate. This motivates control architectures capable of reactive, compliant behavior under combined human and perceptual uncertainty. This paper presents a novel real-time, closed-loop robotic orientation control pipeline for precision visual inspection, with an admittance-based framework that unifies operator input and perception-driven surface alignment. We design the end-effector as a virtual sphere moving through a viscous medium, such that the resulting physically interpretable mass--damper system generates synchronized, compliant motion from orientation error and operator commands. We validate the framework on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrating stable normal-tracking and a final mean orientation error of 0.4°.

</details>


### [204] [SC3-Eval: Evaluating Robot Foundation Models via Self-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18610)
*Wei-Cheng Tseng,Gashon Hussein,Yuzhu Dong,Allen Z. Ren,Lucy X. Shi,XuDong Wang,Sergey Levine,Zhaoshuo Li,Jinwei Gu,Florian Shkurti,Ming-Yu Liu,Quan Vuong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SC3-Eval提出了一种基于自一致性视频生成的机器人策略评估方法，通过前向-逆向动力学一致性、跨视角一致性和测试时一致性三种机制，将预训练视频基础模型转化为准确的策略评估器，在真实世界策略评估中达到0.929的Pearson相关系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界评估通用机器人操作策略成本高、速度慢、难以规模化，而动作条件视频世界模型作为可扩展替代方案面临自回归累积误差、多视角观测一致性以及评估器需泛化到分布外策略行为等挑战。

Method: 提出了SC3-Eval框架，包含三种一致性约束：(1) 前向-逆向动力学一致性——联合训练模型从动作预测帧和从帧恢复动作，将生成轨迹锚定在物理合理的动作流形上；(2) 跨视角一致性——训练模型从其他视角修复每个相机视角，无需显式记忆机制即可保持多视角观测一致性；(3) 测试时一致性——推理时利用逆向动力学模式生成每动作块的不确定性信号，当生成帧偏离请求动作时终止 rollout。

Result: 在七种真实世界视觉-语言-动作策略上，SC3-Eval达到闭环Pearson相关系数0.929和MMRV 0.119，优于三种强基线视频模型方法，并能够泛化到新任务。生成的rollout能够复现策略在真实世界中表现出的失败模式，支持细粒度诊断比较。

Conclusion: SC3-Eval通过三种自一致性机制有效解决了视频世界模型用于策略评估时的累积误差、多视角不一致和分布外泛化问题，为机器人策略的规模化评估提供了一种高效、准确的替代方案。

Abstract: Evaluating generalist robot manipulation policies in the real world is expensive, slow, and difficult to scale. Action-conditioned video world models offer a scalable alternative by simulating policy rollouts. Autoregressive rollouts accumulate compounding errors, observations across multiple camera views must remain mutually consistent, and the evaluator must generalize to policies whose behaviors lie outside the training distribution. We address these challenges with SC3-Eval, a self-consistent video generation recipe that adapts a pre-trained video foundation model into an accurate policy evaluator by enforcing three complementary forms of consistency. First, forward-inverse dynamics consistency jointly trains the model to predict frames from actions and to recover actions from frames, anchoring generated rollouts to a physically plausible action manifold and counteracting the drift a forward-only model cannot penalize. Second, cross-view consistency trains the model to inpaint each camera view from the other, keeping the multi-camera observation coherent over long rollouts without any explicit memory mechanism. Third, test-time consistency reuses the inverse dynamics mode at inference as a per-action-chunk uncertainty signal that terminates rollouts whose generated frames drift away from the requested actions. We also demonstrate SC3-Eval rollouts reproduce the failure modes that policies exhibit in real-world rollouts, supporting fine-grained diagnostic comparison rather than aggregate ranking alone. Across seven real-world vision-language-action policies, SC3-Eval attains a closed-loop Pearson correlation of $0.929$ and MMRV of $0.119$, outperforming three strong prior video-model-based baselines, and generalizes to new tasks.

</details>


### [205] [SRL: Combining SLIP Model and Reinforcement Learning for Agile Robotic Jumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18625)
*Xiaowen Hu,Linqi Ye,Yudi Zhu,Chenyue Shao,Rankun Li,Qingdu Li,Yan Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出弹簧加载强化学习（SRL）框架，将SLIP模型的物理先验与强化学习的自适应能力相结合，用于机器人跳跃控制。实验表明，SRL在双足和四足机器人的地面及楼梯跳跃任务中，能够以更少的训练时间实现更稳定的跳跃，位置跟踪误差低于0.1m，速度跟踪误差在目标值的±3%以内，并在sim-to-sim和sim-to-real验证中展现出良好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLIP模型虽能编码生物启发的弹跳运动，但在不规则地形上因接触和关节动力学的理想化假设而性能下降；RL虽能适应复杂环境，但需要大量无引导探索的数据。两者的互补优势促使提出混合框架以克服各自局限。

Method: 提出弹簧加载强化学习（SRL），将基于SLIP的前馈控制信号与RL驱动的实时反馈相结合，实现对机器人跳跃的持续优化。

Result: SRL相比基线方法能以更少训练时间实现更稳定跳跃，平均位置跟踪误差低于0.1m，速度跟踪误差在目标值的±3%以内。在双足和四足机器人的地面及楼梯跳跃仿真、sim-to-sim和sim-to-real验证中均表现出鲁棒适应性。

Conclusion: SRL框架有效融合了SLIP的物理先验与RL的自适应能力，显著提升了机器人跳跃的稳定性和训练效率，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Robotic jumping is pivotal in applications such as search and rescue and logistics, where crossing obstacles and enhancing mobility efficiency are critical. The Spring-Loaded Inverted Pendulum (SLIP) model leverages simplified spring-mass dynamics that naturally encode biologically plausible hopping motions, yet its performance degrades on irregular terrain due to idealized assumptions regarding contact and joint dynamics. Meanwhile, Reinforcement Learning (RL) can adapt to diverse and complex environments but often requires extensive data from unguided exploration. The complementary strengths of SLIP's physically grounded baseline and RL's adaptive capabilities motivate a hybrid framework that overcomes these individual limitations. We therefore propose Spring-loaded Reinforcement Learning (SRL), which integrates SLIP-based feedforward control signals with RL-driven real-time feedback, enabling continuous optimization of robotic jumping. Experimental results demonstrate that SRL can achieve more stable jumps with much less training time than the baseline method, maintaining an average position tracking error below 0.1 m and velocity tracking errors within +/-3% of the target values. Through bipedal and quadrupedal simulations of ground and stair jumping, as well as sim-to-sim and sim-to-real validations, SRL exhibits robust adaptability to various task requirements and environmental complexities, underscoring its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [206] [Self-Supervised Mask-Aware Transformers for Fault-Tolerant FBG Force Sensing in Minimally Invasive Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18628)
*Peibo Sun,Shiyuan Dong,Shucheng Ye,Jianrong Cai,Yushan Liu,Hongen Liao,Tianqi Huang,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对微创手术机器人中FBG传感器在多通道下的非线性交叉耦合与通道中断问题，提出了一种自监督掩码感知Transformer模型。该模型通过掩码通道预训练、动态损坏课程学习以及异方差不确定性头，在8通道FBG数据集上实现0.0066N的精度，并在4通道失效时仍保持0.0126N，显著优于传统组合模型库方法，且无需模式特定校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术机器人中，FBG传感器虽能通过多通道复用估计多维力，但存在两个关键工程挑战：复杂变形下的非线性交叉耦合以及纤维断裂导致的间歇性通道中断，现有基于组合模型库的容错方法随通道数指数扩展，校准成本过高。

Method: 提出统一的自监督掩码感知Transformer：编码器通过掩码通道重建进行无监督预训练，使用干净与损坏视图联合目标及动态损坏课程进行力回归微调，并设计异方差高斯负对数似然训练的不确定性头实现单次前向传递的每轴置信度预测。

Result: 在8通道FBG数据集上，单统一模型在正常状态下RMSE为0.0066N，4通道严重失效时仅退化至0.0126N，优于包含255个模式特定网络的组合模型库（4通道失效时0.0154N），且无需模式特定校准。

Conclusion: 所提出的自监督掩码感知Transformer能有效统一处理FBG传感器的非线性交叉耦合与通道中断问题，实现优雅退化，为紧凑型多通道传感器的容错力估计提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In minimally invasive surgical robotics, catheter-scale Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) sensors are promising due to their ability to estimate multi-dimensional forces by multiplexing several optical channels. However, deploying these compact multi-channel sensors introduces two critical engineering challenges: inherent nonlinear cross-axis coupling during complex deformations, and intermittent channel dropouts caused by fiber fractures in constrained workspaces. These compounding issues severely degrade force estimation. Existing fault-tolerant approaches rely on combinatorial model banks, which scale exponentially with the channel count and demand prohibitively expensive per-pattern calibration. In this paper, we propose a unified, self-supervised mask-aware Transformer that explicitly models channel availability to enable graceful degradation under diverse and dynamic sensor failures. The encoder is pretrained via masked-channel reconstruction on unlabeled data streams and fine-tuned for force regression using a balanced clean-and-corrupted-view objective alongside a dynamic corruption curriculum. Furthermore, a parallel uncertainty head, trained via heteroscedastic Gaussian negative log-likelihood, predicts per-axis confidence in a single forward pass, circumventing the overhead of multi-pass ensembles. Evaluated on a catheter-scale 8-channel FBG dataset, our single unified model achieves a nominal Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 0.0066~N and degrades gracefully to 0.0126~N under severe 4-channel failures. This significantly outperforms a comprehensive model bank of 255 per-pattern neural networks (0.0154~N at 4-channel loss) while eliminating pattern-specific calibration.

</details>


### [207] [DNN Koopman-Based Deviation Compensation for UGV Path Tracking Control on Coupled Slope and Potholed Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18630)
*Jian Zhao,Wenbo Zhou,Zhicheng Chen,Bing Zhu,Jiayi Han,Dongjian Song,Yinju Lin,Peixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对非结构化环境下无人地面车辆（UGV）路径跟踪控制问题，提出了一种基于深度神经网络（DNN）Koopman的偏差补偿策略。方法包括：自适应遗忘递推最小二乘估计轮胎侧偏刚度、Laguerre模型预测控制（LMPC）降低计算负担、DNN Koopman（DK）路径偏差补偿提升崎岖路面跟踪精度，以及事件触发并行协同（EPC）补偿机制确保指令可行性与车辆稳定性。硬件在环实验显示，所提策略在多种工况下跟踪性能提升超过11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化场景中的复杂地形扰动会显著降低UGV路径跟踪性能，现有方法在补偿崎岖路面扰动方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 基于耦合坡道车辆动力学函数，设计带解耦误差项的自适应遗忘递推最小二乘估计轮胎侧偏刚度；2) 引入Laguerre函数设计LMPC路径跟踪控制器，降低计算资源消耗；3) 结合Koopman算子理论与DNN，提出DK路径偏差补偿方法；4) 建立基于补偿激活准则和可信性验证的EPC补偿机制，耦合LMPC与DK。

Result: 硬件在环实验表明，所提策略在多种工况下跟踪性能提升超过11.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的DK偏差补偿与EPC协同机制能有效提升UGV在复杂非结构化环境下的路径跟踪精度与稳定性，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: Unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) operating in off-road scenarios are confronted with complex terrain disturbances that can substantially degrade path tracking performance. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a deep neural network (DNN) Koopman-based deviation compensation strategy for UGV path tracking control. Firstly, based on the vehicle dynamic function on coupled slope, an adaptive forgetting recursive least squares method with decoupled error terms is designed to estimate tire cornering stiffness. On this basis, a Laguerre model predictive control (LMPC) path tracking control strategy is designed by incorporating Laguerre functions, which can reduce computational resource usage while maintaining reliable tracking performance across different coupled slope scenarios. Then, by integrating Koopman operator theory with DNN, a DNN Koopman (DK) path deviation compensation method is proposed, which significantly improves the path tracking accuracy of UGV under potholed road disturbances. Furthermore, an event-triggered parallel cooperative (EPC) compensation mechanism that couples LMPC with DK is established based on compensation activation criteria and credibility verification. This mechanism improves path tracking accuracy on potholed road while ensuring the feasibility of overall steering command and stability of vehicle after DK compensation. Finally, a hardware-in-the-loop (HiL) experimental platform is constructed for validation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed UGV path tracking strategy improves tracking performance by more than 11.5% across multiple operating conditions.

</details>


### [208] [ROBOSHACKLES: A Safety Dataset for Human-Injury Prevention in Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18632)
*Zhuowen Yin,Chongyang Liu,Wenzhang Yang,Renjue Li,Yinxing Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向具身基础模型（EFMs）的安全关键数据构建流水线，生成了包含10,000个机器人视频片段的数据集ROBOSHACKLES，涵盖直接伤害和间接伤害两类危险场景。评估发现所有被测EFMs在安全关键场景下均产生不安全动作（100%不安全率），该数据集可作为拒绝学习和危险预判的基准与训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型（EFMs）在机器人安全对齐方面研究不足，尤其是涉及机器人伤害人类的真实世界数据无法安全、伦理地采集，亟需一种可扩展的安全关键数据构建方法。

Method: 从真实DROID观测数据出发，通过场景理解、危险感知图像编辑、时间提示生成和单次 rollout 合成四个步骤构建安全关键数据。利用Wan2.7从编辑后的危险状态单次合成逼真的机器人rollout视频，生成10,000个视频片段，涵盖2个直接伤害和4个间接伤害类别。

Result: 构建了ROBOSHACKLES数据集，并通过自动指标评估任务完成度和视觉质量。对6个代表性EFMs进行拒绝式安全评估，结果显示所有模型在测试的安全关键场景下均产生不安全动作，不安全动作生成率为100%。

Conclusion: ROBOSHACKLES可作为EFMs拒绝学习和危险预判的可扩展基准与训练资源，有助于在机器人执行动作前进行安全对齐。

Abstract: Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) integrate multimodal understanding, future-state reasoning, and executable robot actions. Yet their safety alignment for human-injury prevention remains underexplored, primarily because real-world data of robots harming humans or creating hazardous household situations cannot be safely or ethically collected. To address this challenge, we propose a safety-critical data construction pipeline for human-injury prevention in EFMs.Starting from real DROID observations, our construction pipeline proceeds through scene understanding, hazard-aware image editing, temporal prompt generation, and single-pass rollout synthesis. The temporal prompts specify the expected scene evolution, while Wan2.7 synthesizes realistic robotic rollouts from the edited hazardous states in a single pass. Using this pipeline, we construct ROBOSHACKLES, a 10,000-clip robotic video dataset derived from real DROID observations, spanning two direct-harm and four indirect-harm categories. To ensure dataset quality, we assess task completion and visual quality with automatic metrics, and evaluate six representative EFMs under a refusal-based safety criterion. Results show that all evaluated models produce unsafe actions in the tested safety-critical scenarios, yielding a 100% unsafe action generation rate. ROBOSHACKLES serves as a scalable benchmark and training resource for refusal learning and hazard anticipation before robot action execution.The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/YZW00/RoboShackles.

</details>


### [209] [EffiNav: Fusing Depth and Vision-Language for Efficient Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18634)
*Zecheng Yin,Benedict Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiNav框架，通过更智能的探索策略解决未知环境中目标物体导航（ObjNav）的效率问题，在HM3D、OVON和GOAT-BENCH数据集以及真实机器人上验证了其在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）指标上的优越表现，兼顾了鲁棒性、泛化性和实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标物体导航（ObjNav）中，现有基于训练的模型存在泛化性问题，而非训练框架则面临效率低下、易导致重复探索和冗余往返运动的问题。因此，需要一种更高效、鲁棒的导航框架来解决"下一步探索哪里"这一核心挑战。

Method: 提出EffiNav框架，核心在于优化未知环境中的探索决策策略，减少已访问区域的重复探索和不必要的往返运动。该框架仅需少量修改即可扩展至记忆增强型ObjNav任务（GOAT-BENCH）。

Result: 在Habitat Matterport 3D (HM3D)和Open-Vocabulary Object goal Navigation (OVON)两个仿真基准上，以及真实机器人环境中，EffiNav在成功率和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）指标上匹配或超越近期基线方法。通过大规模仿真episode的失败分析验证了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EffiNav在标准ObjNav及其扩展任务中表现出更平衡、更可泛化的性能，兼顾了导航效率和实际部署可行性，适用于搜索救援、现场机器人等真实应用场景。

Abstract: To locate a target object while exploring the unknown environment is a fundamental capability for autonomous agents, with applications ranging from search-and-rescue to field robots. A simplified version of such task is Object Goal Navigation (ObjNav). In ObjNav, successful arrival at the target object provides a basic measure of performance; however, the efficiency of the navigation trajectory is equally important, as it indicates how intelligently the agent explores and how much time remains for subsequent tasks. In unknown environments, the key to efficient navigation lies in deciding where to explore next. While many prior works aim to address this core challenge and achieved promising performance in certain settings, recent training-based models and non-training frameworks still suffer from generalization and efficiency issues respectively, which in the worst cases can lead to excessive exploration of already-visited areas or redundant back-and-forth motion. We evaluate EffiNav on two widely used simulation benchmarks Habitat Matterport 3D (HM3D) and Open-Vocabulary Object goal Navigation (OVON), and further validate its effectiveness on physical robots in real-world settings. We conduct failure analysis on massive simulation episodes. With minimal modification, we also extend EffiNav to a memory-augmented ObjNav task on the GOAT-BENCH dataset, demonstrating its adaptability beyond standard ObjNav settings. Across two standard metrics--Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), EffiNav matches or outperforms recent baselines, reflecting its efficiency, robustness, and practical applicability. Recognizing the different emphases of the two datasets, the performances reveals this framework is more balanced and generalizable for efficient ObjNav.

</details>


### [210] [A Scalable Embodied Intelligence Platform for Seamless Real-to-Sim-to-Real Transfer of Household Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18646)
*Kui Yang,Xianlei Long,Haoxuan Li,Yan Ding,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BestMan是一个可扩展的无缝实-仿-实平台，通过自动化场景生成（ASG）、仿真引导的任务形式化与技能学习架构，以及硬件无关的统一中间件（HUM），解决家庭移动操作中从真实到仿真再回到真实的关键挑战，实现高效策略开发、集成与部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化家庭环境中，移动操作需要鲁棒且泛化的能力，但实-仿-实循环面临高保真仿真场景重建成本高、系统策略评估复杂以及现实部署不兼容三大挑战，亟需一个弥合仿真与现实鸿沟的统一平台。

Method: 提出BestMan平台，包含三个核心模块：(1) 自动化场景生成(ASG)模块，从真实观测中重建逼真仿真场景；(2) 仿真引导的任务形式化与技能学习架构，支持混合技能策略的灵活集成与大规模评估；(3) 硬件无关统一中间件(HUM)，实现跨异构移动操作机器人的无缝实-仿-实迁移。

Result: 实验结果表明，BestMan平台在建立标准化基准和推动移动操作领域研究方面表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: BestMan平台有效解决了实-仿-实循环中的关键挑战，为家庭移动操作提供了可扩展的标准化平台，有望推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a fundamental capability in embodied intelligence robotics. The growing demand for robust and generalizable manipulation in unstructured household environments has driven rapid progress in embodied intelligence platforms. However, achieving a seamless transfer across the real-to-sim-to-real cycle faces three key challenges, including costly high-fidelity simulation scenes reconstruction, the complexity of systematic strategy evaluation in simulation, and incompatible real-world deployments. To address these challenges, we develop BestMan, a scalable and seamless real-to-sim-to-real platform that bridges the gap between the simulation and the real world, enabling effective strategy development, integration, and deployment for household mobile manipulation. Specifically, we design a novel Automated Scene Generation (ASG) module to reconstruct realistic simulations from real observations. Then, we propose a simulation-guided task formalization and skill learning architecture that supports the flexible integration and large-scale evaluations of hybrid skill strategies in simulation. Finally, to enhance the real-world scalability, we develop a Hardware-agnostic and Unified Middleware (HUM) to ensure seamless and compatible sim-to-real transfer across heterogeneous mobile manipulators for real deployments. Experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed platform in establishing standardized benchmarks and facilitating promising research in the field of mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [211] [High-Degree-of-Freedom Lightweight Bioinspired Leg for Enhanced Mobility in Small Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18680)
*Haoqi Han,Yifei Yu,Jiaming Zhang,Xinru Cui,Linxi Feng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受昆虫启发的微型四自由度并联腿机构，通过同心设计策略简化运动学解析，总质量仅18.9g，末端输出力约0.5N，工作空间超22255mm³，实验验证了其优异的运动灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微机器人领域，如何在严苛的空间约束下增加腿机构自由度以提升运动能力仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 采用两个球面五杆连杆机构在并联四杆构型中实现空间运动，并运用同心设计策略简化腿运动学的解析求解；所有执行器位于主体上，减少了运动部件的等效惯量。

Result: 总质量18.9g，末端输出力约0.5N，工作空间超过22255mm³。

Conclusion: 所提出的单腿机构实现了优异的运动灵活性，展示了其在微型仿生机器人中的应用潜力。

Abstract: In microrobotics, enhancing locomotion capabilities by increasing the degrees of freedom (DoF) of leg mechanisms under severe spatial constraints remains a significant challenge. Inspired by insect locomotion, this paper presents a novel micro-scale parallel leg mechanism with four degrees of freedom, and systematically analyzes its mechanical design, electrical system, and kinematics. The design incorporates two spherical five-bar linkages to achieve spatial motion within a parallel four-bar configuration. Furthermore, a concentric design strategy is employed to simplify the analytical solution of the leg kinematics. Due to the parallel system architecture, all actuators are located on the main body, substantially reducing the equivalent inertia of moving parts compared to traditional high-DOF leg structures. The total mass of the system is only 18.9 g, with an end-effector output force of approximately 0.5 N and a workspace exceeding 22255 mm3. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed single-leg mechanism achieves excellent motion flexibility, highlighting its potential for micro bio-inspired robotics.

</details>


### [212] [Leveraging Energy Features for Surface Classification with Deep Learning: A Comparative Analysis Across Three Independent Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18698)
*Alexander Belyaev,Oleg Kushnarev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于能量特征的方法在移动机器人表面分类中的可行性，实验表明单独使用能量特征可达到85-90%的准确率，与惯性特征结合可达96-99%，且作为补充输入可带来1-2%的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量方法在移动机器人表面分类中虽在受限环境下表现良好，但仍未得到充分研究，本文旨在系统评估能量派生特征作为独立分类模态或惯性数据补充输入的可行性。

Method: 在三个公开数据集上，对比了RNN、CNN、编码器-only Transformer及Mamba状态空间模型等现代深度学习架构，并进行了自动化超参数调优和输入序列长度优化。

Result: 所有模型在三个数据集上均取得超过此前报道的准确率；CNN表现最佳；纯能量特征分类准确率为85-90%，低于结合惯性特征的96-99%；惯性数据补充能量特征可带来1-2%的均值准确率提升。

Conclusion: 仅依赖能量特征的分类器足以独立部署，且与其他感知模态结合时可带来一致的性能增益。

Abstract: The energy-based method remains a comparatively underexamined approach for surface classification in mobile robotics, despite promising results in constrained environments. This study evaluated the viability of using energy-derived features as either a standalone classification modality or as supplementary input to inertial data. A comprehensive evaluation was conducted across three publicly available datasets, comparing the performance of modern deep learning architectures including recurrent neural networks, convolutional neural networks, encoder-only transformers, and Mamba state-space models, under automated hyperparameter tuning and input sequence length optimization. The models achieved higher accuracy than previously reported values on all evaluated datasets, with the convolutional neural network yielding the highest overall performance. When relying exclusively on energy-based features, the models attained classification accuracies in the range of 85-90%, approximately 5-10% lower than those achieved when combined with inertial features (96-99%). Augmenting inertial data with energy features resulted in a consistent mean accuracy improvement of 1-2%. These findings indicate that classifiers relying solely on energy features offer sufficient accuracy for standalone deployment, while also providing a consistent gain when used in combination with other sensing modalities.

</details>


### [213] [Selective Unit-Cell Actuation in Lattice Structures for Distributed Morphology in Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18704)
*Trevor Exley,Altair Coutinho,Lucia Beccai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将气动驱动集成到晶格单胞尺度的嵌入式气动单元，通过空间驱动模式而非均匀加压实现全局形态变化，实验证明了可扩展变形、弯曲抓取和爬行运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体晶格结构通常采用被动架构后置驱动器的方式，缺乏在单元尺度上实现驱动-结构协同设计的方案。

Method: 提出嵌入式气动单元，将弯曲支柱晶格几何与双向波纹管驱动器集成于单个整体单元中，通过空间驱动模式控制全局形态。

Result: 1×1、2×2和3×3拼装实验验证了可扩展的位移和力产生及可重复循环性能；3×3×3阵列中选择性驱动实现了弯曲和定向抓取；主动/被动单元耦合实现了弯曲驱动的爬行运动。

Conclusion: 单元级驱动策略能够实现晶格软体机器人的分布式变形，为可扩展单片机器人架构奠定基础。

Abstract: Soft lattice structures are increasingly used in robotics to tailor compliance and guide deformation; however, actuation is typically introduced at the device or module level, with actuators inserted into otherwise passive architectures. In this work, we move actuator-lattice co-design to the unit-cell scale. We present an embedded pneumatic unit cell that integrates curved-strut lattice geometry with a bidirectional bellow actuator within a single monolithic element. When tessellated, the lattice functions as a distributed actuation field in which global morphology is governed by spatial actuation patterns rather than uniform pressurization. Experimental characterization of 1x1, 2x2, and 3x3 tessellations demonstrates scalable displacement and force generation with repeatable cyclic performance. Selective actuation of unit cells in a 3x3x3 array produces distinct global deformation modes, including bending and directional grasping, without altering hardware configuration. Additionally, coupling active and passive unit cells enables bending-driven crawling locomotion, demonstrating that heterogeneous tessellations can translate through asymmetric deformation. These results establish unit-cell-level actuation as a strategy for distributed morphing in lattice-based soft robots and provide a foundation for scalable, monolithic robotic architectures.

</details>


### [214] [Two-Phase Bilevel Search for the Moving-Target Traveling Salesman Problem with Moving Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18730)
*Allen George Philip,Anoop Bhat,Sivakumar Rathinam,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了带移动障碍物的移动目标旅行商问题（MT-TSP-MO），提出了混合整数锥规划（MICP）公式和两阶段双层搜索（TPBS）算法，在最多40个目标和40个障碍物的实例上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MT-TSP研究未考虑移动障碍物约束，而实际问题中（如无人机、自动驾驶）障碍物普遍存在，因此需要将问题泛化到MT-TSP-MO并设计有效求解方法。

Method: 提出两种方法：1）MICP公式化，可利用现成求解器求解；2）TPBS算法，一种快速可扩展的两阶段双层搜索方法，用于生成高质量可行解。

Result: 在多达40个目标和40个障碍物的广泛实例上，两种方法在成功率、解成本和计算时间三个方面均显著优于现有基线算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的MICP和TPBS方法有效解决了带移动障碍物的MT-TSP问题，兼顾了求解精度与计算效率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The Moving-Target Traveling Salesman Problem (MT-TSP) seeks a minimum cost trajectory for an agent that departs from a static depot, visits a set of moving targets, each within one of their assigned time windows, and returns to the depot. In this article, we study the Moving-Target Traveling Salesman Problem with Moving Obstacles (MT-TSP-MO), a generalization of the MT-TSP where the agent trajectory must avoid moving obstacles. We present a Mixed-Integer Conic Programming (MICP) formulation that can be solved using off-the-shelf solvers, as well as a fast and scalable Two-Phase Bilevel Search (TPBS) algorithm that computes high-quality feasible solutions for the problem. We evaluate our approaches against an existing baseline algorithm on a broad range of problem instances with up to 40 targets and 40 obstacles. The results demonstrate that both the proposed methods significantly outperform the baseline with respect to success rates, solution costs, and computation time.

</details>


### [215] [Generating Natural and Expressive Robot Gestures through Iterative Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18747)
*Chris Lee,Flora Salim,Benjamin Tag,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将ChatGPT集成到人形机器人Pepper中生成伴随语音的 gestures，并引入迭代强化学习与人类反馈（RLHF）系统来优化手势生成，实验表明RLHF能显著提升LLM生成手势的表现力、相关性和流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人（如Pepper）需要产生自然且富有表现力的手势以提升人机交互质量和长期接受度，但现有方法依赖专家动画（僵化不灵活）或机器学习（难以捕捉自然度且随自由度增加而困难），缺乏能够适应环境同时遵循社交规范和物理约束的手势生成系统。

Method: 将ChatGPT集成到Pepper机器人中实现从自然语言动态生成伴随语音的手势；进一步提出迭代强化学习与人类反馈（RLHF）系统，基于用户评估对手势生成进行微调优化。

Result: RLHF改进了LLM的伴随语音手势生成能力，生成了更具表现力、相关性和流畅性的动作。

Conclusion: 基于LLM结合RLHF的方法能有效提升社交机器人手势生成的自然度和表现力，为人机交互中动态、自适应的手势合成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Expressive gestures are essential for natural and effective communication, complementing speech when verbal cues alone are insufficient (e.g., pointing). For social robots such as the humanoid Pepper, producing natural and expressive movements is critical for improving human-robot interaction (HRI) and long-term acceptance. However, generating gestures remains challenging due to reliance on expert-authored animations, resulting in rigid behaviors that are impractical for dynamic and diverse environments. Alternatively, machine learning approaches often struggle to capture perceived naturalness, becoming increasingly challenging with more degrees of freedom. Consequently, producing expressive robot gestures requires a system that can adapt to the environment while adhering to social norms and physical constraints. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable dynamic code generation, offering new opportunities for runtime gesture synthesis from natural language. In this paper, we integrate ChatGPT into the humanoid robot Pepper to generate co-speech gestures aligned with conversational output. While this baseline enables flexible gesture generation, the resulting motions are often perceived as stiff and unnatural. To address this limitation, we introduce an iterative reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF) system that finetunes gesture generation based on user evaluations, leveraging an iterative user study to compare Pepper's generated gestures. Our results show that RLHF improved the LLM's co-speech generative capabilities, producing more expressive, relevant and fluid movements.

</details>


### [216] [HALOMI: Learning Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Active Perception from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18772)
*Zehui Zhao,Yuxuan Zhao,Gaojing Zhang,Chenxi Liu,Maolin Zheng,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALOMI是一个从人类演示中学习人形机器人全身协调操控的框架，通过扩展UMI加入自我中心感知、流形约束控制器和跨域对齐技术，在Unitree G1机器人上实现了85%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据虽能自然捕捉手眼协调行为，但直接迁移到人形机器人面临两大挑战：(1) 传统世界帧跟踪控制器在分布外目标下表现脆弱；(2) 人与机器人之间在自我中心观察和动作执行方面存在显著的领域差异。

Method: (1) 扩展UMI框架，加入自我中心感知（ego-view和wrist-view），实现头-手轨迹数据大规模采集；(2) 提出流形约束控制器，在学习的潜在行为流形中规划，实现精确鲁棒的世界帧头-手跟踪；(3) 通过自我中心视图对齐和控制器感知的参考轨迹适配，弥合人与机器人之间的观察与执行差距。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上完成5类真实世界任务（导航、抓取、双臂操作、全身协调、动态行为），三个定量评估任务平均成功率达85%，并展示了动态抛掷和深蹲抓取等定性行为。

Conclusion: HALOMI有效解决了从人类演示到人形机器人的跨域迁移问题，通过流形约束控制和对齐技术实现了鲁棒的全身协调操控，为人形机器人学习提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Human demonstrations, which can be collected at scale and naturally capture active hand-eye coordination, are a promising data source for learning humanoid loco-manipulation. However, directly transferring human demonstrations to humanoids requires a precise world-frame tracking controller, which is often brittle under Out-of-Distribution(OOD) targets, while human-to-humanoid gaps persist in both egocentric observation and action execution. To address these challenges, we present HALOMI, a scalable framework for learning humanoid loco-manipulation with active perception from human demonstrations. HALOMI extends Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) with egocentric sensing to collect ego-view and wrist-view observations along with head-hand trajectories at scale. We further propose a manifold-constrained controller that plans in a learned latent behavior manifold to enable precise and robust head-hand tracking in the world frame. To bridge the human-to-humanoid gap, we perform ego-view alignment and introduce a controller-aware reference trajectory adaptation to reduce mismatch in both observation and action execution. We validate HALOMI on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot with an actuated neck across five real-world tasks involving navigation, grasping, bimanual manipulation, whole-body coordination, and dynamic behaviors. Across the three quantitatively evaluated tasks, HALOMI achieves an average success rate of 85\%, while additional qualitative demonstrations show its ability to support dynamic tossing and deep-squat grasping.

</details>


### [217] [Space Is Intelligence: Neural Semigroup Superposition for Riemannian Metric Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18828)
*Chenghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能置于空间本身而非智能体中：场景在构型流形上诱导黎曼度量，行动变为沿测地线运动，无需独立规划器或碰撞检测器。通过编码器-路由器网络实现，三个参数组通过半群-叠加机制生成单一黎曼度量场，架构简洁且随场景复杂度自然扩展。在单障碍场景上训练后，展现出对未见障碍配置的鲁棒零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将智能置于智能体内部（学习策略或搜索过程），本文试图颠覆这一范式，将智能赋予空间本身，使行动成为几何空间的固有属性，从而简化规划流程。

Method: 提出编码器-路由器（Encoder-Router）网络，包含三个互补参数组：帧参数（orient generators）、调制参数（govern spatial propagation）、基本系数（determine strength），三者通过共享的半群-叠加机制生成单一黎曼度量场。该网络在单二障碍场景上训练。

Result: 训练于单二障碍场景的模型展现出对未见障碍配置的鲁棒零样本泛化能力，无碰撞路径成本与穿透障碍路径成本之间呈现数量级级别的分离。

Conclusion: 将智能置于空间本身的几何方法有效可行，紧凑架构随场景复杂度自然扩展，有望替代传统基于搜索或学习的路径规划方法。

Abstract: Traditional approaches place intelligence in the agent, whether as a learned policy or a search procedure. We instead place intelligence in the space itself: a scene induces a Riemannian metric on the configuration manifold, and action reduces to following the geodesics of that metric rather than invoking a separate planner or collision checker. A single Encoder-Router network realizes this idea through three complementary parameter groups -- frame parameters that orient the generators, modulation parameters that govern their spatial propagation, and basic coefficients that determine their strength. These groups combine through a shared semigroup-superposition mechanism to produce a single Riemannian metric field, yielding a compact architecture whose geometry scales naturally with scene complexity. Trained on a single two-obstacle scene, the model demonstrates robust zero-shot generalization across unseen obstacle configurations, with orders-of-magnitude separation between collision-free and obstacle-penetrating path costs.

</details>


### [218] [ZiMPedance: Impedance-Aware ZMP Modeling and Control for Payload Carrying with Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18883)
*Giovanni B. Dessy,Lorenzo Amatucci,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对四足机器人负载运输中被动弹簧臂引起的动力学问题，推导了包含被动负载-接口动力学的扩展ZMP公式，并基于此设计了融合被动子系统动力学的MPC控制器，在仿真中减少10倍稳定性违规，硬件实验验证了抗扰动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人负载运输中，被动弹簧臂虽能减轻重量和复杂度，但其弹簧-阻尼动力学产生的振荡力会降低 locomotion 稳定性，现有控制方法未充分考虑这种被动接口动力学的影响。

Method: 1) 推导了包含被动负载-接口动力学的扩展ZMP公式，建立刚度、阻尼、负载质量与稳定裕度的关系；2) 将被动子系统动力学增强到单刚体动力学(SRBD)模型中；3) 将增广模型集成到模型预测控制(MPC)框架中。

Result: 仿真中稳定性违规从7.0%降至0.7%（减少10倍），水平地面反作用力降低15%。硬件实验中2kg负载下成功抵抗拉拽释放扰动，而基线控制器失败。

Conclusion: 融合被动接口动力学的MPC控制能有效抑制被动弹簧臂产生的振荡扰动，提升四足机器人负载运输的稳定性和效率，且无需直接驱动被动臂即可实现末端执行器跟踪。

Abstract: Load transportation with quadruped robots is strongly affected by the dynamics of the physical interface between the robot and the load. Passive spring-based arms reduce weight and complexity compared to active manipulators, but their spring-damper dynamics can introduce oscillatory forces that degrade locomotion stability. This paper derives an extended Zero Moment Point (ZMP) formulation that includes passive payload-interface dynamics, relating stiffness, damping, and payload mass to the stability margin. The analysis shows that underdamped configurations can resonate with locomotion harmonics. Based on this insight, we augment a Single Rigid Body Dynamics model with passive subsystem dynamics and integrate it into a Model Predictive Control framework. In simulation, the proposed controller reduces stability violations by up to $10\times$, from $7.0\%$ to $0.7\%$, and increase locomotion efficiency by lowering horizontal ground reaction force effort by up to $15\%$ compared to a nominal baseline. Hardware experiments with a $2\,\mathrm{kg}$ payload show stable locomotion under pull-release disturbances where the nominal controller fails. The same model also enables end-effector tracking through passive arm dynamics without direct arm actuation.

</details>


### [219] [C-ARC: Continuous-Adaptive Range Clustering for Non-Repetitive LiDAR Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18948)
*Nick B. Schroeder,Jonathan Lichtenfeld,Oskar von Stryk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出C-ARC，一种适用于非重复式LiDAR（如Risley棱镜传感器）的连续自适应范围聚类框架。该框架通过滑动窗口上的持久双图结构解耦高频点插入与按需聚类检索，并采用自适应范围网格分辨率机制平衡稀疏性与碰撞。在Livox Mid-360上实现20Hz实时聚类，开源实现为单线程C++17库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非重复式LiDAR传感器（如Risley棱镜式）因成本低、体积小而被广泛应用，但其Rhodonea曲线轨迹产生非均匀点分布，且缺乏旋转周期导致传统扫描线索引失效。现有方法多针对重复式机械LiDAR开发，无法直接适用于这类传感器。

Method: 1) 持久双图结构：在滑动窗口上维护持续更新的图结构，将高频点插入与按需聚类检索解耦。2) 自适应范围网格分辨率：通过指数控制循环在初始化时校准网格维度，无需扫描模式先验知识即可权衡稀疏性与碰撞。

Result: 在Livox Mid-360上实现20Hz实时聚类输出；在Livox Avia上发现无界单元占用是扫描模式高度集中传感器的主要限制；自适应分辨率机制能提升现有基于网格方法在非重复数据上的聚类质量。

Conclusion: C-ARC有效解决了非重复式LiDAR聚类的关键挑战，为SLAM、跟踪等应用提供了实时可行的方案，且自适应机制具有通用性。

Abstract: Real-time LiDAR clustering identifies structures in point clouds, which is an essential prerequisite for many mobile robotics algorithms. Current methods are mostly developed for repetitive mechanical LiDAR sensors. Recently, the use of non-repetitive LiDAR sensors is strongly increasing due to their small cost and form factor. Such non-repetitive Risley prism-based sensors violate two key assumptions of repetitive mechanical sensors: structured scan lines and well-defined frame boundaries. Their Rhodonea-curve trajectories produce non-uniform point distributions, and the absence of a rotation cycle renders conventional scan line indexing inapplicable. To meet such new requirements, we developed C-ARC, a Continuous-Adaptive Range Clustering framework that maintains a persistent dual-graph over a sliding window, decoupling high-frequency point insertion from on-demand cluster retrieval. This is crucial for key functionalities like SLAM or tracking. An adaptive range grid resolution mechanism calibrates grid dimensions at initialization using an exponential control loop, balancing the sparsity-collision trade-off without prior knowledge of the scanning pattern. Implemented as an open-sourced single-threaded C++17 library, C-ARC produces real-time cluster output at 20 Hz on commodity hardware for the Livox Mid-360. Evaluation on the Livox Avia identifies unbounded cell occupancy as the primary limitation for sensors with strongly concentrated scan patterns. The adaptive resolution mechanism additionally improves clustering quality for existing grid-based methods on non-repetitive data.

</details>


### [220] [A High-accuracy Event-based Underwater SLAM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18951)
*Yifan Peng,Qihang,Liu,Haoying Li,Yuzhe Li,Junfeng Wu,Ziyang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于事件相机的高精度水下立体SLAM系统，通过结构感知的Time Surface指标、贝叶斯优化与异步在线搜索相结合的TS生成策略、先验视差约束和"最新观测优先"三角化机制，解决了现有方法在水下场景中因相机速度波动、宽基线纹理重复导致的成像质量差和匹配失败问题，并贡献了首个高质量水下事件数据集UWE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Time Surface的事件SLAM方法在水下环境中性能极不可靠：相机速度波动严重降低TS成像质量，宽立体基线与重复水下纹理导致关键匹配失败并频繁引发系统崩溃，亟需针对水下场景设计专门的解决方案。

Method: 1) 基于结构张量相干性和梯度设计结构感知的TS量化指标；2) 将最优TS生成解耦为两阶段：初始化前使用贝叶斯优化预测最优先验TS，跟踪阶段使用异步在线局部搜索周期性地实时获取合适TS；3) 利用先验视差保证精确数据关联，采用"最新观测优先"三角化机制实现稳定三角化；4) 贡献UWE水下事件数据集。

Result: 在公开数据集和UWE数据集上的广泛评估表明，所提出的SLAM系统在精度上优于现有最先进的事件SLAM方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效克服了水下场景中相机速度波动和纹理重复等挑战，实现了高精度的事件立体SLAM，且代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: While event cameras offer immense potential for underwater SLAM, existing Time Surface (TS)-based methods prove highly unreliable when deployed underwater. Fluctuating camera velocities severely degrade TS imaging quality, while wide stereo baselines and repetitive underwater textures induce critical matching failures, frequently triggering system failure. To overcome these challenges, we develop the first high-accuracy event-based underwater stereo SLAM system. A structure-aware metric for TS is designed based on structure tensor coherence and gradients to quantitatively evaluate TS structural information density. By decoupling the optimal TS generation into two distinct stages based on system initialization, Bayesian Optimization(BO) first predicts an optimal prior TS sequentially before initialization while we set an asynchronous online local searching method periodically to obtain appropriate TS in real-time during the tracking stage. We use the prior disparity to guarantee precise data association and "latest-observation-first'' triangulation mechanism to realize stable triangulation. As a benchmark for these solutions and a resource for the community, we also contribute UWE, the first high-quality real-world underwater event dataset containing variable camera motions, complex textures and different trajectory features. Extensive evaluations on public datasets and UWE show the competitive accuracy performance of the proposed SLAM system compared to the state-of-the-art event-based method. The code and data will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [221] [Object-Centric Residual RL for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real VLA Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18953)
*Kinam Kim,Namiko Saito,Heecheol Kim,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Jaegul Choo,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以物体为中心的残差强化学习框架，通过在仿真中训练残差策略来零样本提升真实世界VLA模型的鲁棒性，在Franka FR3机器人上五种操作任务中将成功率从42%提升至76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型基于模仿学习在精确物理交互中因累积执行误差而表现脆弱；现有残差RL方法面临仿真到现实的困境：基于特权状态的方法需要有损蒸馏，基于图像的方法存在视觉域差距，而真实世界RL成本高且不安全。

Method: 提出物体中心残差RL框架，使用物体位姿作为紧凑观测空间以一致地在仿真与现实间迁移；通过在仿真中重放相同的遥操作演示来训练仿真VLA对应模型；残差RL策略仅在仿真中训练，采用位姿噪声注入和丢弃技术，零样本迁移到真实机器人。

Result: 在Franka Research 3机器人上五种操作任务中，该方法零样本地将成功率从42%提升至76%，且改进后的轨迹可进一步用于重新训练基础VLA模型实现自我改进。

Conclusion: 物体中心残差RL框架有效解决了仿真到现实的迁移难题，通过紧凑的物体位姿观测空间和仿真内训练策略，实现了零样本的鲁棒性提升和闭环自我改进。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can generalize across diverse manipulation tasks, but their imitation-learning-based policies remain brittle in precise physical interactions due to compounding execution errors; Can a reinforcement learning policy trained purely in simulation improve the robustness of real-world VLAs zero-shot? Residual RL, which learns a corrective policy on top of a frozen VLA, offers a natural framework, but existing approaches face a fundamental sim-to-real dilemma: privileged-state methods require lossy distillation for deployment; image-based methods suffer from the visual domain gap; and real-world RL is costly and unsafe. We propose an object-centric residual RL framework that refines VLA actions using object poses, enabling a compact observation space that transfers consistently between simulation and reality. To align the two domains, we additionally replay the same teleoperation demonstrations in simulation to train a sim counterpart of the real-world VLA. The residual RL policy is trained only in simulation with pose noise injection and dropout, and transfers zero-shot to the real robot. Across five manipulation tasks on a real Franka Research 3 (FR3) robot, our method improves the success rate from 42% to 76% zero-shot, and the improved rollouts can be further reused to retrain the base VLA for self-improvement without additional teleoperation. Project page: https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/articles/object-centric-residual-rl/

</details>


### [222] [TactSpace: Learning a Physics-enriched Shared Latent Space for Tactile Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18959)
*Arunim Joarder,Arjun Bhardwaj,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Florin Püntener,Sira Bielefeldt,Cosmin Roman,Vaishakh Patil,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态表示学习框架，将异构触觉模态对齐到共享潜空间，实现触觉感知的零样本仿真到真实迁移，在力预测和形状重建任务上分别降低16.7%和45.8%的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仿真器无法高保真建模触觉传感器的复杂形变与换能机制，严重阻碍了机器人学习中的仿真到真实迁移。

Method: 构建模态特定编码器将不同触觉观测（如仿真穿透深度、真实电容）投影到公共嵌入空间，采用自重建、交叉重建与对比对齐联合训练，学习模态不变且信息丰富的表示。

Result: 在仿真训练、真实测试的零样本迁移中，力预测误差降低16.7%，形状重建误差降低45.8%。

Conclusion: 多模态表示学习可有效弥合触觉传感器仿真与真实之间的差距，多物理场仿真模态能生成更具信息量的嵌入表示。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides direct measurements of contact interactions that are essential for robotic manipulation. However, current simulators lack the fidelity to faithfully model the complex deformation and transduction mechanics of tactile sensors, severely hindering sim-to-real transfer in robot learning pipelines. To address this challenge, we propose a multi-modal representation learning framework that aligns heterogeneous tactile modalities within a shared latent space, eliminating the need for accurate raw-signal simulation while preserving relevant contact information. Our approach employs modality-specific encoders to project diverse tactile observations, such as simulated penetration depth and real-world capacitance, into a common embedding space. The model is trained using self- and cross-reconstruction objectives alongside contrastive alignment, encouraging modality-invariant yet information-rich representations. We evaluate the learned embeddings on indenter shape identification, force prediction, and geometric reconstruction tasks, training exclusively in simulation and testing directly on real sensor measurements. Our results demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across physically dissimilar representations. Furthermore, incorporating multi-physics simulation modalities yields more informative embeddings that transfer across diverse downstream tasks, demonstrating a 16.7% reduction in force prediction error and a 45.8% reduction in shape reconstruction error. Finally, we release an efficient Warp-based implementation of a penalty-based tactile simulation model for Isaac Lab, enabling scalable tactile data generation.

</details>


### [223] [Congestion-Aware Robot Tour Planning in Crowded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19031)
*Stefano Bernagozzi,Charlie Street,Masoumeh Mansouri,Lorenzo Natale*

Main category: cs.RO
